### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Task2Hand_A_Contact-Grounded_Skill_for_Parametric_Dexterous-Hand_Morphology_Design|Task2Hand: A Contact-Grounded Skill for Parametric Dexterous-Hand Morphology Design]]

![[assets/2611.27967_figure.jpg|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27967)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27967
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Task2Hand_A_Contact-Grounded_Skill_for_Parametric_Dexterous-Hand_Morphology_Design|Task2Hand: A Contact-Grounded Skill for Parametric Dexterous-Hand Morphology Design]]
- **作者**: Haoyang Wang, Weibo Gao, Zhengxiong Li, Lingfeng Tao
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 2.1（加权：具身智能 1.5，大模型 0.1，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Task2Hand: A Contact-Grounded Skill for Parametric Dexterous-Hand Morphology Design》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DERL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Dexterous hand design demands repeated expert work across geometry, robot-description code, collision debugging, grasp initialization, and policy training. We present Task2Hand, a Skill: a specification package that turns a functional manipulation brief into a parameterized hand model and routes every candidate through cost-ordered contact, dynamics, and learning gates. Each gate returns a status and an evidence record; on failure, the Skill records the earliest blocker, revises one bounded parameter group, and reruns the cheaper gates. The agent generates, validates, and trains autonomously within declared bounds, and escalates trade-offs, plateaus, and conflicting evidence to a human who sets the next design direction. An evidence-driven design trajectory for bidirectional in-hand manipulation yielded SAGE Hand (Skill-Aware Generative Embodiment): four fingers, 16 actuated DoF, an integrated guiding palm, compact roots, cylindrical tips, and a collision-cleared opposable thumb. Under a matched training protocol, SAGE Hand reaches 45.6% task success on a hexagonal prism against 37.3% for the LEAP hand, and 70.8% on a bicolor cylinder against 51.6%. Dense simulated tactile input more than halves the cylinder drop rate. In a controlled iteration study, the Skill path preserves capture at 164/165 trials while the path without the Skill degrades to 14/165, confirming that the gates act as a regression guard during refinement. Accepted candidates pack with URDF/MJCF, metrics, checkpoints, and a provenance manifest.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Humanoid_robots_for_safety-critical_electrical_switching_A_perception-informed_runtime_constraint_filter|Humanoid robots for safety-critical electrical switching: A perception-informed runtime constraint filter]]

![[assets/2611.27845_figure.jpg|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27845)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27845
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Humanoid_robots_for_safety-critical_electrical_switching_A_perception-informed_runtime_constraint_filter|Humanoid robots for safety-critical electrical switching: A perception-informed runtime constraint filter]]
- **作者**: Songyang Liu, Yu Hou, Yuqing Hu, Shuai Li
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.8（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《Humanoid robots for safety-critical electrical switching: A perception-informed runtime constraint filter》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Humanoid robots can reduce personnel exposure in electrical switching, but safe control is challenging because the tool must reach a target switch next to similar devices, where an imprecise operator command can contact a non-target switch. This study proposes a runtime safety filter that checks each operator command during virtual-reality teleoperation of a humanoid robot. The filter integrates a scene model estimated from the robot head camera and a target-conditioned control barrier function quadratic program that keeps the tool away from the non-target switch while passing safe commands unchanged. In real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot, with six rounds at each of two standing positions, target operations succeed in all ten rounds with an unchanged target and no non-target actuation. A simulation study achieves 9/9 safe completions, compared with 5/9 for raw teleoperation, demonstrating the feasibility of the method for humanoid switching operations.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/EvoTER_Evolving_Embodied_Agents_via_Tool-Use_Experience_and_Reflection|EvoTER: Evolving Embodied Agents via Tool-Use Experience and Reflection]]

![[assets/2611.27797_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27797](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27797)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27797
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/EvoTER_Evolving_Embodied_Agents_via_Tool-Use_Experience_and_Reflection|EvoTER: Evolving Embodied Agents via Tool-Use Experience and Reflection]]
- **作者**: Yingting Zhou, Yuyin Zhou, Yongliang Wang, Haoran Li, Dongbin Zhao
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 大模型, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.3（加权：具身智能 1.5，大模型 0.5，机器人 0.3）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《EvoTER: Evolving Embodied Agents via Tool-Use Experience and Reflection》归入 具身智能、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CheckVLA, ProactiveVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Long-horizon embodied tasks require agents to ground instructions in changing observations, coordinate heterogeneous tools, and recover from failures that can propagate through downstream operations. Although existing LLM-based agents retain experience and refine reusable skills, successful local repairs do not by themselves establish complete-task gains over the current shared procedure library. We present EvoTER, an experience-driven framework that enables embodied agents to self-evolve tool-use skills without updating model parameters. EvoTER operates through a three-role evolution loop: Explorer records tool-use trajectories; Diagnoser distills successful and failed executions into skills for tool selection, parameter computation, postcondition checks, and recovery; and Validator retains candidate revisions only when they improve complete-task success in fresh sessions. In simulation, EvoTER improves overall task success from a pre-evolution baseline of 53.60% to 80.40%. In real-world experiments, simulation initialization reduces the attempts required to acquire short-horizon skills, while continued real-world evolution improves long-horizon success from 20% to 80%-100% across tasks and enables the evolving skill library to incorporate new tool functionality introduced after simulation development. Project page: https://evoter-project.github.io/

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/An_Autonomous_Adaptive_Learning_System_for_Robotic_Grasping_in_the_Circular_Factory|An Autonomous Adaptive Learning System for Robotic Grasping in the Circular Factory]]

![[assets/2611.27782_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27782)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27782
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/An_Autonomous_Adaptive_Learning_System_for_Robotic_Grasping_in_the_Circular_Factory|An Autonomous Adaptive Learning System for Robotic Grasping in the Circular Factory]]
- **作者**: Yitian Shi, Rosa Wolf, Rania Rayyes
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.8（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《An Autonomous Adaptive Learning System for Robotic Grasping in the Circular Factory》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Linear manufacturing typically operates in highly structured settings with standardized objects, allowing robots to execute pre-programmed motions based on precise models. In contrast, circular manufacturing involves dynamic and less controlled environments, where product components can vary due to appearance and physical changes across different product generations. In this context, uncertainty arises from both the unknown or partially observed states of components (e.g., damaged surfaces, altered shapes) and the unstructured disassembly environment, often characterized by heavy clutter, occlusions, and limited sensing viewpoints. This demands a flexible and adaptable robotic grasping system that can cope with unexpected object changes. To address these challenges, this paper proposes an autonomous grasp learning framework designed for highly uncertain and variable conditions. The framework explicitly distinguishes between uncertainty due to noisy or incomplete sensory observations (aleatoric uncertainty) and uncertainty due to limited knowledge and distribution shift (epistemic uncertainty). Moreover, the framework employs an active perception strategy to reduce visual ambiguity: semantic and spatial scene understanding is achieved using vision language models (VLMs), while view planning enables the robot to adjust its viewpoint to improve observability. In addition, the approach leverages large-scale human demonstration data to learn interaction patterns, which are then transferred to robotic end-effectors, enabling robust manipulation in these variable settings. Quantitative validation, including the utility of active vision on disassembled components in practice, is expected to form an important part of future work.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied-Harness_Towards_Recursive_Self-Improvement_for_Robotic_Manipulation|Embodied-Harness: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement for Robotic Manipulation]]

![[assets/2611.27689_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27689)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27689
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Embodied-Harness_Towards_Recursive_Self-Improvement_for_Robotic_Manipulation|Embodied-Harness: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement for Robotic Manipulation]]
- **作者**: Zhenxuan Fan, Zhuoya Wang, Yuqian Yuan, Wenqiao Zhang, Juncheng Li, Jun Xiao, Siliang Tang, Yueting Zhuang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 4.5（加权：具身智能 2.7，大模型 0.5，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Multimodal, Agent

#### 研究背景与动机

《Embodied-Harness: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement for Robotic Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AgiBot-World, RMBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language model (VLM)-based agents offer a promising approach to robotic manipulation, yet remain limited by inadequate memory organization, uncertain failure attribution, and limited reuse of execution experience. We propose Embodied-Harness, a dual-agent framework that improves manipulation by refining the execution harness without updating model parameters. The Actor interacts with the environment through a robot-control interface, while the Reflector converts execution evidence into reusable operational guidance. Hierarchical Memory Architecture maintains short-term execution context and long-term experiential knowledge to support execution and reflection. Evidence-based Experience Reflection grounds failure diagnosis in observed interactions, while Recursive Harness Self-Improvement refines operational guidance across successive attempts. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, RoboSPA, and RMBench demonstrate the effectiveness of Embodied-Harness in generalization under environmental variations, spatial reasoning, long-horizon planning, and memory-dependent manipulation. Our method achieves success rates of 82.4%, 76.7%, and 94.4% on the three benchmarks, respectively. Real-world evaluation further yields an average success rate of 96.7% across three manipulation tasks. These results highlight the potential of Embodied-Harness to improve robotic manipulation through experience-driven harness self-improvement.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Trust_What_Repeats_Capped_World-Model_Policy_Gradients_for_Humanoid_Motion_Tracking|Trust What Repeats: Capped World-Model Policy Gradients for Humanoid Motion Tracking]]

![[assets/2611.27648_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27648)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27648
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Trust_What_Repeats_Capped_World-Model_Policy_Gradients_for_Humanoid_Motion_Tracking|Trust What Repeats: Capped World-Model Policy Gradients for Humanoid Motion Tracking]]
- **作者**: Xuzhe Dang, Stefan Edelkamp
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 3.0（加权：具身智能 1.5，强化学习 0.2，世界模型 0.2，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: RL, WorldModel

#### 研究背景与动机

《Trust What Repeats: Capped World-Model Policy Gradients for Humanoid Motion Tracking》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Robotics 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ProCuRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Training a humanoid motion tracker by backpropagating imagined returns through a learned world model is fast, because the model gives a first-order policy gradient. We ask how much of that gradient holds in the simulator. On a tracker for the Unitree G1, the $0.5\%$ of imagined rollouts with the largest gradients carry $22\%$ of the summed gradient norm, yet neither a second rollout from the same state nor the simulator confirms them. When the actions are moved along the model's gradient, the simulator returns 94% of the predicted change in return where the gradient norm is below the batch median, 48% between 3 and 10 times the median, and 1% above 30 times; the part of a gradient that either check confirms stops growing at a few times the median. We therefore cap each rollout's action-gradient norm at 10 times the batch median, draw imagination starts in proportion to their predicted gradient norm so that the cap removes less, and reset episodes where the world model and critic predict the lowest return. Trained on 223 hours of motion and tested on 353 motions that no actor performed in training, the method raises the normalized return of SuperMimic, the baseline it builds on, from 0.953 to 0.967 and the lowest $1\%$ of test clips from 0.65 to 0.76, halves the test clips that fall persistently, from 15--18 to 7--10, and reaches the baseline's final return in 40% of the baseline's iterations and half its training time. On LAFAN1, trained and evaluated on the same clips as prior work, it reaches 0.935 against 0.906.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Efficient_VLA_Reinforcement_Learning_via_Subchunk_Rollouts_in_Latent_World_Models|Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Subchunk Rollouts in Latent World Models]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Subchunk Rollouts in Latent World Models》归入 具身智能、世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Ctrl-World, Latent-RL, MBRL, PlaNet, RLT-RL, SimpleVLA-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) can improve pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies through imagined experiences. However, existing VLA-MBRL methods primarily rely on large-scale image or video generation world models, incurring large computational overhead. Latent world models offer a more efficient alternative but face two challenges: compounding errors during recursive prediction and incompatibility with VLAs' visual input interfaces. In this work, we propose LS-MBPO, a lightweight residual MBRL framework that leverages latent world models to improve upon pretrained VLAs. LS-MBPO introduces two key techniques: (1) Hierarchical Forcing, which combines dense causal supervision with autoregressive feedback to mitigate compounding prediction errors; and (2) Latent Subchunk Rollouts, which leverage action chunks from a frozen VLA policy and a latent residual policy for successive subchunk refinement, bridging the gap between latent imagination and VLA policy execution. We further introduce a Progress-Conditioned Branch-Start Curriculum that progressively shifts rollout starting points from later to earlier task stages, enabling effective policy learning from short imagined rollouts. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and a real-world ALOHA robot show that LS-MBPO achieves superior task performance and training efficiency compared to existing methods.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/S-BFM_A_Behavioral_Foundation_Model_for_Humanoid_Control_from_Sparse_Spatiotemporal_Goals|S-BFM: A Behavioral Foundation Model for Humanoid Control from Sparse Spatiotemporal Goals]]

![[assets/2611.27552_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Kun Yang, Xuanyu Chen, Jing Tan, Yanjing Liu, Yubo Zhao, Renjing Xu, Addison Lin Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《S-BFM: A Behavioral Foundation Model for Humanoid Control from Sparse Spatiotemporal Goals》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

General-purpose whole-body trackers can execute diverse humanoid motions, but they require dense reference trajectories. However, tasks usually specify only where, in which posture, and when the robot should arrive, leaving the motion in between to a separate planner. Inspired by human motor coordination, our key idea is that a low-level controller can organize the intervening motion and its pace by itself from sparse goals with arrival times. In this paper, we propose S-BFM, a novel behavioral foundation model superior in its autonomy: it generates whole-body transitions from target postures, poses, and arrival times alone. Specifically, we first introduce Spatial Grounding, which adds world-frame position and heading to a forward-backward behavior representation through spatial reconstruction. Then, Time-Conditioned Distillation is designed to learn transitions and pace from real motion through closed-loop imitation of a dense-reference teacher. Lastly, Generative Fine-Tuning is introduced to broaden the covered distances and arrival times with a distilled motion generator. In simulation, S-BFM lowers global joint-position error by over 32% relative to the best baseline under fixed and mixed horizons, and raises fixed-goal reach from 43.5% to 74.6% while cutting terminal position error from 1.67 to 0.26 m. We further deploy S-BFM on the full-size AGIBOT A3 humanoid. Sparse spatiotemporal goals thus suffice for natural, well-timed whole-body motion.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《ROSWORLD: When Digital Task Competence Meets Physical Execution》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AndroidWorld, EmbodiedBench, Meta-World, OSWorld, OpenVLA, RLBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Computer-use agents now solve most desktop tasks through direct software input, but a robot using the same computer must turn GUI knowledge into physical contact with a keyboard and mouse. We introduce ROSWorld, a benchmark that couples a physically simulated robot workstation to a live OSWorld desktop, so that a robot and a desktop agent pursue the same goals under the same official evaluators. The evaluated model receives observations only from the workstation camera views and operates the peripherals through either a single-arm rigid probe or dual-arm dexterous hands. Across five frontier models and 40 tasks in ten applications, models that complete 31-39 tasks on the desktop complete at most 23 with the probe and 28 with the hands. Paired trajectory analysis shows that models adapt their interaction strategies toward keyboard input, yet physical execution frequently fails through missed presses, incorrect targets, and unintended contacts. ROSWorld makes the gap between knowing what to do on a computer and physically doing it measurable.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Parametric body models expose incompatible pose and shape controls, tying humanoid policies to particular input formats. AnyBodyCodec learns a shared representation through native conversion, then reuses it for new body models and control with its core frozen. Encoders map MHR, SMPL-X, and SOMA to aligned slots; native decoders recover target controls. The 1.090M-parameter converter covers nine routes and reaches 16.801 mm input-reference MPJPE across six cross routes: the 2.887M Transformer bank reaches 16.390 mm and its parameter-matched version 18.866 mm. Three measured routes take 7.43-7.67 ms on an RTX 4090. Adding ANNY shows that comparable conversion accuracy can accompany different action consistency under one frozen controller. Under a matched PPO budget, aligned slots support 60.78% full-action success across three formats. Separate 160-hour training reaches 66.10% source-weighted validation success, and the controller is deployed qualitatively on a physical G1 through three visual front ends. Together, these results establish reuse through both native geometric fidelity and downstream policy compatibility.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27338_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27338](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27338)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27338
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Latent_Jacobian_Steer_Eliciting_Contact-Rich_Out-of-Distribution_Behaviors_from_Pretrained_Humanoid_Controllers|Latent Jacobian Steer: Eliciting Contact-Rich Out-of-Distribution Behaviors from Pretrained Humanoid Controllers]]
- **作者**: S K Swaminathan, Shivam Sood, Shreyansh Kansal, Chengyang He, Guillaume Adrien Sartoretti
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Latent Jacobian Steer: Eliciting Contact-Rich Out-of-Distribution Behaviors from Pretrained Humanoid Controllers》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DEP-RL, OpenVLA, Real2Sim, SPiRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Given a pretrained general latent-conditioned whole-body humanoid controller, say a behavioral foundation model (BFM), how can we elicit complex and contact-rich out-of-distribution behaviors? Raw joint-action exploration enables direct, task-specific adjustments but can disrupt coordination learned by the pretrained controller, whereas latent exploration retains that coordination but is difficult to steer toward desired physical effects. To combine the complementary strengths of these exploration spaces, we propose Latent Jacobian Steer (LJS), which couples sampling in direct joint-action space with the semantics of the learned frozen controller's input latent space. For a task specified through desired physical effects, we sample task-relevant joint-action perturbations and map them through a damped local inverse of the policy Jacobian to obtain candidate latent ($z$) commands. These commands are executed through the frozen pretrained policy, while exploration is directed toward the physical outcomes required by the new task. Candidate outcomes are evaluated in simulation and composed into latent-command sequences that accomplish the task. Ablations show that neither action-space nor latent-space exploration alone provides the same capability as their coupling. Across diverse out-of-distribution tasks, simulation-based searching using this method produces contact-rich behaviors, including hurdling, manipulating articulated objects, and opening doors through non-prehensile contact. Over ten seeds, LJS completes more of each humanoid task than when searching in either space alone. Beyond humanoids, it also steers a goal-conditioned dexterous hand policy through four assembly tasks better than searching in goal space directly.

</details>

《Embodied Interaction Policy: A Unified Model for Full-Duplex Human-Robot Interaction》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Real-time human-robot social interaction requires continuous coordination between speech and physical behavior. Existing systems often separate interaction agents from motion generation, complicating coordination as conversational states change. We propose \textbf{Embodied Interaction Policy (EIP)}, a unified streaming framework that couples an Audio-Interaction expert with a motion expert through layer-wise joint attention. This allows motion generation to directly access the evolving interaction context. A chunk-wise causal mask prevents access to future context, while a vocal token encodes speaking state and the semantic emotion of the response. We construct \textbf{Embodied Interaction Dataset (EI-Data)} from co-speech and dyadic interaction data and train our model in two stages to learn state-adaptive and emotion-conditioned behavior. Combined with the frozen interaction expert, EIP supports continuous robot motion during proactive responses and interruption-aware turn-taking. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate improved motion quality over representative gesture-generation baselines. Compared with a representative cascaded system, EIP achieves higher interaction success and reduces warm-start latency by 64\%. Physical deployment and user studies further demonstrate improved motion naturalness and interaction comfort.

</details>

<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent embodied agents construct and adapt robot behavior through executable programs, tool use, and interaction feedback. Studying these systems in mobile manipulation calls for physical worlds that connect agent-directed execution with its physical consequences and reusable interaction experience. We introduce $γ$-Scape, an agent-centric simulation and benchmark platform for mobile embodied intelligence. Agents compose motion tools and learned skills to carry out complete tasks that combine navigation and manipulation in a persistent physical world. BenchForge grounds structured objectives in physical task instances with instructions, reference programs, and goal evaluators, defining task goals while preserving agents' choice of valid solutions. An agentic collection harness further enables agents to produce demonstrations by carrying out contact-rich interactions through sustained control and physical feedback. Assisted reference execution and human guidance provide complementary experience. The resulting tool traces and aligned observation-action records support program refinement and policy learning, with common task-goal criteria for subsequent evaluation. We benchmark representative agent systems on complete tasks and demonstrate contact collection and trace-informed program revision. Together, these workflows provide infrastructure for research on embodied recursive self-improvement.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/LiDAR_Signals_for_Robot_Locomotion_Spherical_Range_Images_Enable_Robust_Sim-to-Real_Transfer|LiDAR Signals for Robot Locomotion: Spherical Range Images Enable Robust Sim-to-Real Transfer]]

![[assets/2611.27223_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Nian Liu, Xinyi Tong, Ming Ren, Long Ye, Zhaofeng He
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI

#### 研究背景与动机

《LiDAR Signals for Robot Locomotion: Spherical Range Images Enable Robust Sim-to-Real Transfer》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：PointNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Existing legged-locomotion systems commonly rely on depth images or online elevation maps. However, depth-based policies suffer from perceptual sim-to-real discrepancies due to imperfect depth sensor modeling, while map-based approaches are vulnerable to drift and misalignment. LiDAR offers direct measurements of the surrounding geometry, but raw LiDAR scans lack a compact, structured representation for high-frequency control. We propose a LiDAR perception pipeline that maintains perceptual consistency across simulation and reality through sensing- and representation-level alignment. At the sensor level, we recover frame-wise angular sampling patterns from the physical LiDAR and replay them in simulation with GPU ray casting. At the representation level, simulated and real returns undergo the same spherical range image encoding, yielding a fixed-size policy observation for end-to-end training and direct deployment without teacher--student distillation, online elevation mapping, or adaptation. Experiments show that our range-image policies achieve stronger sim-to-real transfer than depth- and elevation-map-based baselines, while ablations confirm the importance of the proposed sensing components. More visualizations and real-world videos are available at the \href{https://lidar-img.github.io/}

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SCAP_Stochastic_Context_for_Action_Expert_Pretraining|SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining]]

![[assets/2611.27187_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27187](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27187)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27187
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SCAP_Stochastic_Context_for_Action_Expert_Pretraining|SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining]]
- **作者**: Xiao Lin, Yaoxu Lyu, Ronghao Dang, Zhili Liu, Kaijing Ma, Yang Zhou, Dingning Liu, Runzhe Teng, Jun Cen, Shanghang Zhang, Chengju Liu, Qijun Chen
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：BayesVLA, LA4VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Single-modality action-expert pretraining controls competition from visual shortcuts before vision--language--action (VLA) adaptation. Yet single-modality pretraining can impair the vision--language interactions needed for subsequent joint prediction. We construct a diagnostic model in which VA or LA pretraining can disable an initially active pathway for joint vision--language modeling. In this model, subsequent training with complete visual and language inputs cannot restore the pathway, even with unlimited adaptation. We therefore introduce Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining (SCAP), a new action-expert pretraining stage that precedes full-input training. During this stage, visual and text tokens are randomly masked before entering the VLM. In this unified diagnostic model, SCAP can preserve the joint vision--language modeling pathway during pretraining and subsequent full-input training. Compared with direct full-input training, it can also reduce reliance on visual shortcuts and increase reliance on language instructions. SCAP consistently improves performance across four different VLA architectures. In real-robot experiments, it achieves 76.67% average success across three instruction-conditioned manipulation tasks, outperforming full-input pretraining by 25 percentage points.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/HumanoidPose_Monocular_3D_Pose_Estimation_for_Humanoid_Robots|HumanoidPose: Monocular 3D Pose Estimation for Humanoid Robots]]

![[assets/2611.27181_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27181)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27181
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/HumanoidPose_Monocular_3D_Pose_Estimation_for_Humanoid_Robots|HumanoidPose: Monocular 3D Pose Estimation for Humanoid Robots]]
- **作者**: Jinlin Gan, Mingzhe Li, Junsheng Zhang, Zhibo Zhou, Jiahan Geng, Yuhua Luo, Changwang Zhang, Chenglu Wen, Lan Xu, Siqi Shen, Cheng Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.8（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《HumanoidPose: Monocular 3D Pose Estimation for Humanoid Robots》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World, RoboNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

As humanoid robots increasingly enter real-world environments, recovering their 3D whole-body pose from monocular imagery is becoming increasingly important for external state perception, motion analysis, and robot learning. However, Humanoid Robot Pose Estimation (HRPE) lies at the intersection of conventional Robot Pose Estimation (RPE) and Human Pose Estimation (HPE), while not being fully addressed by either paradigm: RPE primarily focuses on non-humanoid robots, particularly fixed-base manipulators, whereas HPE is built upon human-specific skeletal topologies, body models, and motion priors. Despite recent progress in humanoid 2D keypoint detection and HPE adaptation, humanoid-native 3D pose recovery remains insufficiently studied, and a dedicated benchmark with suitable training data and evaluation protocols is still lacking. To address this gap, we introduce Humanoids, a benchmark for monocular 3D humanoid robot pose estimation, and propose HumanoidPose, a framework for this task. Humanoids provides synthetic 3D supervision across diverse humanoid embodiments, manually annotated real-world 2D data, and real-world RGB sequences paired with robot states for 3D evaluation on a physical Roban humanoid. HumanoidPose combines human-inspired visual perception with robot articulation modeling and explicit kinematic grounding. Experiments on Humanoids and the DHRP-H1 benchmark demonstrate strong performance in articulation recovery and 3D pose estimation, including zero-shot transfer to real-world 2D and 3D evaluation. Ablation studies further show the contribution of robot-native articulation modeling and explicit kinematic grounding. The dataset and code will be publicly released to support future research in the community.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning from Incomplete Actions: Interval Motion Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Policies》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visual labeling of egocentric demonstrations often leaves contiguous gaps in action supervision. Reliable poses around a gap still provide its net translation and rotation. We use this information to train vision-language-action policies while preserving ordinary flow matching on reliable local actions. A composition objective constrains the aggregate motion of missing-action estimates, combining multiple fixed windows when a gap exceeds the policy horizon. A shared interval branch learns from reliable endpoints and supplies the composition target. On LIBERO with 20% of demonstrations and 70.5% missing actions, the main method achieves 87.9% mean success over three training runs. In one paired comparison with matched observation exposure and training cost, its advantage over masking is 10.5%. On Ego-informed RoboTwin gaps, success is 45.4%, versus 40.3% for masking. On five real-robot tasks, mean success is 59.6%, versus 54.8% for interval flow matching alone and 48.8% for masking.

</details>

- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《Mem-X: A Memory-Dependent Cross-Episode Benchmark for Vision-and-Language Navigation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GOAT-Bench, LMEE-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Long-term memory enables embodied agents to reuse past observations across tasks. However, existing cross-episode vision-and-language navigation (VLN) benchmarks often leave it unclear whether successful navigation relies on historical memory or current exploration. We introduce Mem-X, a benchmark designed to evaluate memory-dependent cross-episode VLN. Mem-X organizes episodes into sessions in which subsequent instructions refer to objects or routes encountered in earlier episodes. Its three levels evaluate historical-object recall, in-memory localization and episodic indexing, and off-memory relocalization and historical-route reproduction. For the latter two levels, agents receive episode-local poses but no transformations between episode frames, requiring them to establish spatial correspondence with historical memory. Mem-X comprises 2,071 episodes across 710 sessions in 34 scenes, including 1,204 memory-dependent episodes. We also provide MemBridge, a modular baseline that combines semantic scene memory, cross-episode localization, and coarse-to-fine retrieval to support existing VLN agents. Experiments with state-of-the-art VLN methods demonstrate the benefits of MemBridge while revealing substantial room for improvement on memory-dependent navigation. The benchmark and baseline code are available at https://github.com/NJU-R-L-Group-Embodied-Lab/Mem-X.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《TrajRealm: A Large-Scale Point Tracking Dataset with Diverse Dynamics》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EBench, OmniNavBench, TrajRealm-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

General-purpose point tracking requires large-scale training data with diverse dynamics, yet existing datasets struggle to combine rich dynamic content, accurate scene geometry, and sufficient scale. We introduce TrajRealm, the first public synthetic point-tracking dataset to reach the ten-million-frame scale, constructed from games and embodied simulations. TrajRealm uses the rich environments and motion systems of modern games to capture diverse targets, activities, and interactions across diverse worlds, while embodied simulations add robot manipulation and indoor navigation data. The dataset provides RGB observations, depth, camera parameters, and persistent 2D and 3D point trajectories. These annotations provide precise, geometrically consistent supervision across foreground objects and static environmental surfaces. We also build TrajRealm-Bench with eight subcategories under Interaction and Locomotion to evaluate tracking across diverse scenes. Benchmark evaluations reveal the strengths and limitations of existing trackers in handling complex motion and maintaining long-term point correspondences. Training on TrajRealm improves the performance of existing tracking methods on synthetic and real-world benchmarks.

</details>

- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习
- **相关性评分**: 2.2（加权：具身智能 1.5，强化学习 0.2，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《PACE: Policy Augmentation and Compensation With Explicit Disturbance Estimation for Robust Locomotion》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning has made quadrupedal locomotion remarkably robust. To handle unmodeled dynamics and external disturbances, domain randomization is usually employed to make a policy hedge against every condition it might meet. However, maintaining accurate tracking across varying dynamics and generalizing beyond the sampled range remain challenging. Here we show that explicitly estimating the disturbance and feeding the resulting estimation back into both the policy and the controller can relax this compromise. We introduce Policy Augmentation and Compensation with Explicit disturbance estimation (PACE). The framework lumps payload variations, center-of-mass (CoM) shifts, actuator degradation, and external forces into one joint-space disturbance. A nonlinear observer subsequently recovers this disturbance online without requiring joint acceleration measurements. The disturbance estimation provides feedforward torque compensation as the foundational control pathway and further augments the policy observation to enable disturbance-aware action generation. Considering the learning difficulty of fast-varying contact effects, we further provide an analytical contact estimation approach from joint and inertial measurements, without demanding force sensors. Across eleven simulated dynamics conditions, PACE reduces velocity-tracking error by 18.1% to 57.0% against state-of-the-art baselines, and maintains improved tracking under out-of-distribution (OOD) commands. Notably, in real-world experiments, the deployed real robot carried an 11.5 kg payload, nearly equal to its own mass. It also negotiated unseen 16 cm step-downs and crossed five outdoor terrains. The policy was trained exclusively on flat ground in simulation with at most 3 kg of added mass.

</details>

- **作者**: Zhaoshan Yang, Chenglei Li, Haoyu Lei, Yutao Zhang, Yuyu Yang, Zhuo Chen, Ling Ding, Zhongxing Ren, Yi Gu, Heyang Zhang, Peng Su, Jin Pan

#### 研究背景与动机

《BeyondEgo: Learning to Act Beyond Egocentric Perception via Humanoid Ego-Exo Foresight》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Humanoid loco-manipulation requires coordinating whole-body motion to perform manipulation tasks in complex environments. Yet egocentric vision-language-action (VLA) policies can miss critical body--scene relationships, and action imitation alone provides no explicit supervision for future scene evolution. We propose BeyondEgo to extend VLA perception in space and time. Spatially, we encode a synchronized exocentric view with frozen DINOv3 and concatenate the resulting tokens with egocentric VLM tokens to condition the action expert. Temporally, we use future observations from both views as privileged supervision for a residual adapter on task-conditioned VLM features. A foresight expert regresses DINOv3 representations of both views 0.6s ahead. At deployment, the foresight expert is discarded and the 4.2M-parameter adapter is retained. We also introduce HELM, to our knowledge, the first closed-loop humanoid loco-manipulation benchmark with synchronized egocentric and exocentric views across five whole-body tasks.On HELM, BeyondEgo improves success from 48.0% to 92.0% in distribution and from 23.5% to 65.0% on 200 held-out scenes with layout, asset, material, and lighting shifts. Ablations show that dual-view DINOv3 foresight yields the best transfer results.

</details>

- **作者**: Chenghao Yin, Da Huang, Di Yang, Jichao Wang, Junhui Wu, Nanshu Zhao, Tong Ran, Zhijun Li, Gang Zhao, Linting Guo, Rui Feng, Sheng Zhang...

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboColiseum: Co-evolution of Simulation, Evaluation, and Models》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EmbodiedBench, EmbodiedEval, Real2Sim, VLABench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Simulation-based evaluation of embodied models requires calibrated interactions, diverse tasks, and explicit execution constraints. We present RoboColiseum, a platform combining system identification, composable physics and rendering, and multidimensional benchmarking. Recorded-command replay characterizes robot dynamics and object interactions, while a shared interface connects rigid and deformable simulation to rendering. Across 22 paired real--simulation robot--task configurations, task scores have a reported Pearson correlation of 0.9686. Eight evaluation tracks span instruction following, spatial reasoning, general manipulation, robustness, dexterous manipulation, precision manipulation, whole-body coordination, and execution across robot embodiments. Budget-aware scoring combines task progress, resource use, and completion rewards. We report 12 selected Season~I policies and all 11 systems on the public Season~II leaderboard, comprising eight Agent systems and three VLA policies. Agent2Act connects language models to robot-control execution; the VLA study uses mixed-task supervised fine-tuning. The results reveal substantial variation across tracks within each evaluation category. RoboColiseum connects simulation development with capability-oriented evaluation and provides a foundation for extending robot interactions and model assessment.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27011_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27011)

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《FoRe: A Force-Guided Framework for Autonomous Grasp Recovery in Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoT-VLA, CycleVLA, OpenVLA, ReCoVLA, Real-World, SpatialVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance in grasp-based manipulation, yet reliable recovery from local grasping failures remains challenging. Specifically, in pick-and-place tasks, policies may continue transport after a failed grasp instead of retrying, potentially reflecting overreliance on proprioceptive cues for stage progression and limited retry supervision. Existing recovery approaches typically rely on deployment-time guidance or demonstration augmentation. We introduce Force-guided Recovery (FoRe), a framework for autonomous recovery from local grasping failures. Using force feedback, FoRe partitions demonstrations into approach-grasp and transport-placement stages to train separate policies. To supplement limited retry supervision, FoRe then initializes a dedicated retry policy from the approach-grasp policy and refines it through local reinforcement learning (RL) from post-failure states. To avoid inappropriate stage progression, a controller combines force feedback with grasp-command status at inference to coordinate the stage policies, detect grasp failures, and invoke the retry policy. We also construct LIBERO-SUCTION, a force-augmented suction manipulation benchmark adapted from LIBERO, with complete demonstration trajectories for policy training. FoRe consistently improves task success rates for VLA-Adapter and $π_{0.5}$ on LIBERO-SUCTION, LIBERO, and real-world tasks. On LIBERO-SUCTION, average absolute gains are 18.4% and 6.5%, respectively. The corresponding real-world absolute gains are 25.0% and 21.7%. FoRe also remains effective with only one demonstration per task on both simulation benchmarks.

</details>

![[assets/2611.26969_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26969)

- **作者**: Zhiyuan Li, Rui Heng Yang, Xuan Zhao, Amir Rasouli
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.9（加权：具身智能 1.8，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboDyna: A Benchmark for Evaluating Robotic Manipulation under Physically Grounded Dynamics》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ReflexBench, World-in-World, X-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Object manipulation in the dynamic real world requires understanding how the environment functions and evolves, and acting accordingly. Existing benchmarks, however, do not adequately address these needs. Physics-oriented benchmarks evaluate predictive fidelity but not closed-loop action. Dynamic robotic manipulation benchmarks often use simplified, physically implausible object motions, limiting evaluation of reasoning and action under realistic dynamics. Evaluation is also typically synchronized, freezing the environment during inference and making computation time irrelevant to task outcomes. To this end, we propose RoboDyna, a benchmark for evaluating the physical understanding and action capabilities of manipulation policies in dynamic scenes. RoboDyna comprises 20 conceptual dynamic tasks spanning five capability categories, with simple objects and minimal scene clutter, under a two-factor difficulty design, and 10 household tasks that instantiate the same capabilities in visually and semantically richer simulated real-world environments. RoboDyna provides 3,500 expert demonstrations, paired synchronous and asynchronous evaluation protocols, metrics for task success and partial completion, and interactive tools for human baseline evaluation. Evaluating vision-language-action models and a world-action model across 5.4K episodes, we find that all policies achieve success rates below 20%, revealing substantial limitations in dynamic manipulation. The world-action model captures simple physical dynamics but struggles with complex interactions, and its prediction fidelity does not consistently align with task success. Sync-async comparisons show that inference latency flips 5-12% of episode outcomes while changing average success rates by at most 3%. Human baselines substantially outperform all policies under matched conditions. Project page: https://robodyna.github.io/robodyna/

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Analysis of Multi-Client Inference Techniques for Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) are a new class of machine learning model for generalizable robot control. While they enable robots to perform complex tasks in unseen environments, serving VLAs leaves hardware idle between inference passes and under-utilized during inference. Serving multiple clients at once can improve utilization, but fundamental multi-client techniques such as batching and co-execution have not been characterized for VLAs. We therefore characterize the trade-offs these known techniques impose across latency, throughput, power, and hardware utilization. Our findings inform trade-offs and challenges for future multi-VLA serving systems.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EventVLA, MemoryBench, OpenVLA, RMBench, Real-World, VMBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robotic manipulation often requires remembering visual information that is no longer observable, such as the original location of an object. Existing approaches typically rely on full observation histories, which become redundant over long horizons, or text summaries, which may omit precise visual details. We study visual moment memory, the ability to retain such details from key moments for subsequent manipulation. To support this study, we introduce VMBench, a suite of long-horizon manipulation tasks requiring memory of spatial locations, visual appearances, and their associations. We further propose MomentMem, a hierarchical architecture in which a VLM-based memorizer selects keyframes and an executor predicts actions conditioned on the selected memories. This separation enables us to evaluate memory selection alongside closed-loop task success and to study memorizer generalization independently of the executor. On VMBench, MomentMem achieves 49.9% average success, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 31.7 percentage points. Moreover, we find that the memorizer transfers well to unseen appearances zero-shot. It can also adapt to an unseen task with only a few memory annotations, reaching 43.7% success, close to the 44.3% obtained with full memory supervision. Further experiments show competitive performance on RMBench and improvements in real-robot manipulation. These results highlight the value of retaining sparse visual evidence for memory-dependent manipulation. Our code is available at https://github.com/PKU-VaLuE-Lab/MomentMem.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《FORTH: Grounding Tactile VLA Execution via Forward-Only Proxy-Control Pairing》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are widely credited with force control, operating on the unverified assumption that proxy loss minimization implies physical force grounding. In this work, we demonstrate that this assumption breaks down at three core deployment levels: frozen visual backbones discard contact geometry, downstream losses mask token-level gradient alignment, and discrete indicators trigger optimization collapse. To address these disconnections, we present FORTH, a forward-only measurement harness and execution framework that explicitly pairs the optimization objective at each level with its physical control observable. FORTH incorporates three grounded pillars: a layer-local, forward-only tactile encoder that preserves spatial contact geometry without backpropagation, a token-parallel zeroth-order adapter enabling activation-space updates, and SlipGuard, a continuous tactile verification gate that fires on incipient slip. On an elasto-plastic structural shell, FORTH's closed-loop gate peaks at 29 times lower grip force than a monotone reflex ramp, preventing structural crush where the baseline leaves permanent deformation. Furthermore, FORTH matches state-of-the-art execution performance with a 97.6% average success rate across three evaluated LIBERO suites, while reducing peak activation memory by 7.12 times compared to a backpropagated step on the same backbone. Enhanced perception enables deeper physical intelligence.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Search-RSI_Expanding_Search_Worlds_Along_Multimodal_Agents'_Capability_Frontier|Search-RSI: Expanding Search Worlds Along Multimodal Agents' Capability Frontier]]

![[assets/2611.27780_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27780)

- **作者**: Chengchao Lv, Yin Wang, Chunzheng Zhu, Wanli Chang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型, 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Search-RSI: Expanding Search Worlds Along Multimodal Agents' Capability Frontier》归入 大模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：VQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multimodal search agents answer knowledge intensive visual questions by grounding them in retrieved evidence, and recent recipes train them on multi hop tasks that are synthesized from knowledge graphs and grown online to keep pace with the agent. These recipes, however, treat the searchable world as fixed: a new question can be well formed and verifiable while its evidence is absent from the retrieval backend, so the agent issues the right query, retrieves nothing, and is penalized as if its policy were at fault. Such unreachable negatives accumulate with every round of growth. We propose Search-RSI, a recursive self improvement framework that expands the search world along the agent's capability frontier. In each round, the current policy diagnoses its partially mastered regions of a task space spanned by hop depth, relation rarity, and distractor density; new tasks there are grown together with their evidence; and the same policy decides how they are used: candidates on which its probes disagree feed reinforcement learning, while those beyond its reach become hint guided demonstrations of its own. Keeping the policy inside this loop matters: an improver frozen at the start of training loses 3.2 points. On nine multimodal search benchmarks, Search-RSI keeps improving where static task growth saturates, raising the average accuracy of the recipe it builds on by 9.8 and 9.5 points at 9B and 27B, of which 7.0 points are out of reach for a world that is merely made consistent. The loop uses no external model and only privileged task structure that is removed before training, at 1.27x the compute of static growth.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

There has been significant recent interest in understanding the behaviour of LLM agents from the perspective of human preferences or corpus statistics. In this paper we propose agent states as intermediate variables through which post-training can be measured and predicted. From a statistical physics perspective, we study the emergence of a global potential which governs the transition of state in LLM agent reasoning. We introduce a partition function to model the ideal KL-regularised transition distribution and track potential structure, mean reward and state entropy. We demonstrate our approach in two Qwen3.5-9B tasks: safety-test selection and physics-reasoning auditing. We observe that the potential structure strengthens during post-training as predicted from our framework, while joint changes in reward and state entropy provide consistency evidence. States thus provide a quantitative description of how post-training organises agent behaviour.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Bringing AI Assistance to the Beznau Nuclear Power Plant: A Hierarchical System of LLM Agents for Reactor Monitoring》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Beznau Unit 1 in Switzerland, in operation since 1969, is the most experienced nuclear reactor still running worldwide. Five decades of incremental modernization have left its monitoring systems, alarm logs, variable catalogs, technical specifications and investigation reports in separate silos that were never designed to be queried together. As a result, investigating a core instrumentation anomaly means slow, manual gathering of knowledge scattered across databases, documents, and experienced colleagues. We present a hierarchical multi-agent assistant built on large language models (LLMs) to support these investigations and reduce the need for tedious manual searches in SAP systems. An orchestrating agent breaks down an engineer's question and hands the parts to specialized sub-agents, each with read-only access to one source. Domain expertise is written as natural-language procedures that plant engineers can read, review and revise. The system is strictly advisory, with every answer traced back to its sources. We also report organizational lessons from introducing agentic AI into a culture built on safety, traceability and conservative change. Beyond the technical architecture, the Beznau case study examines how an assistant can be introduced into a setting where evidence must be traceable, changes carefully reviewed, and operational decisions retained by plant personnel, paving the way for the responsible use of agentic AI in industrial plant monitoring.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Local-cloud agent systems pair a cloud model with a small on-device agent that acts on private data, often as the last check before an irreversible action. We identify, for the first time, a failure in which such an agent is led into a violation by its own correct decisions within a session. After it correctly releases an item whose restriction was lifted, it becomes more likely to release the next item that is still restricted. Because the trigger is a correct decision, a history with no faulty step can still end in a violation. We call this failure correct-precedent generalization and find it in 13 open-weight models from nine developers. A single correct precedent can cut the safe rate of the most affected agent roughly in half, and the risk compounds when a refused request is simply sent again in a new session. Interventions on the agent's history indicate that it carries its decision on the released item over to the restricted one instead of checking that item's status. Building on this finding, we propose Paired Decision Distillation (PDD), which distills the decisions of a weakly affected teacher into a LoRA adapter. PDD restores the safe rate to nearly 100% in every distilled model, without extra prompt text or model calls.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Do_Senior_Agents_Do_Their_Job_Measuring_Role_Integrity_in_How_LLM_Agents_Use_Subordinate_Material|Do Senior Agents Do Their Job? Measuring Role Integrity in How LLM Agents Use Subordinate Material]]

![[assets/2611.27139_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Jinxiang Xie, Xinyi Zhang, Changjiang Jiang, Kexu Liu, Qi Yang, Yuxin Su
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Do Senior Agents Do Their Job? Measuring Role Integrity in How LLM Agents Use Subordinate Material》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CoRL, RI-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly divide long tasks among subagents whose reports a senior agent turns into the final answer. Such a division of labor pays off only if each agent, and above all the senior agent, carries out the duties of its role. Current evaluations, however, mostly score the final answer, and a fluent, well-cited answer cannot show whether the senior agent weighed its subagents' reports or merely aggregated them. To assess whether a senior agent meets its assigned responsibilities, we define role integrity as the preservation of an agent's functional responsibilities when it acts on its subordinates' material. To measure it, we introduce RI-Bench, a behavioral benchmark grounded in the structures and public records of three institutional hierarchies: peer review, appellate adjudication, and parliamentary inquiry. We choose these hierarchies because their rules explicitly assign the senior role a duty toward subordinate material, and together they cover three basic duties: to weigh, to verify, and to relay. Rather than scoring output correctness, we measure how senior agents' outputs respond to controlled changes in subordinate material, using rule-based checks and a rubric-guided LLM judge calibrated against human annotation. Across eight LLMs and over 12,000 agentic episodes, senior agents rarely adopt a planted procedural error (2.9% of rulings) but often follow a majority of generic reviews (up to 71% of decisions) or drop witnesses' stated limitations (up to 65% of cited claims). Obligation prompts reduce these failures without removing them, and they also shift behavior in the control conditions. These findings motivate direct, obligation-specific evaluation of role integrity rather than inferring it from general capability or explicit role instructions. Code and data are available at https://github.com/jxtse/ri-bench.

</details>

- **arXiv**: [2611.27046](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27046)

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Actions_Speak_Louder_than_Words_Stateful_Privacy_Sniffing_of_LLM_Agents_via_Behavioral_Covert_Channels_in_Web_Interactions|Actions Speak Louder than Words: Stateful Privacy Sniffing of LLM Agents via Behavioral Covert Channels in Web Interactions]]
- **作者**: Ruichao Liang, Wenzhong Deng, Jing Chen, Yuqing Li, Kaixuan Li, Cong Wu, Yang Liu
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 0.9（加权：大模型 0.9）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Security

#### 研究背景与动机

《Actions Speak Louder than Words: Stateful Privacy Sniffing of LLM Agents via Behavioral Covert Channels in Web Interactions》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：URL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM agents increasingly rely on Web interaction, issuing searches, following links, and reading pages, to complete complex user tasks. Since each action is driven by the agent's private context, including the user's prompt, dialogue history, memory, and connected data, it also opens a new privacy exposure surface. Prior attacks either passively analyze network traffic, which yields only coarse correlations, or hijack the agent through indirect prompt injection, which must place sensitive content in an outgoing field and is blocked by content-level defenses. Whether an attacker can actively recover fine-grained private context without any explicit transmission thus remains insufficiently understood. Our insight is that an agent's benign Web decisions themselves leak private context, as what it chooses to visit encodes whether, which, and in what order it acts, forming a behavioral covert channel. Building on this, we design SNOOP, a stateful hierarchical probing framework that narrows a coarse-to-fine hypothesis tree per task and reconstructs user queries and profiles from the agent's observable paths. Across four agents and three LLM backbones on 500 StackExchange queries and 378 multi-turn iKAT dialogues, SNOOP recovers query concepts with an average F1 of 0.89 and user profiles with an F1 of 0.80, while four representative agent defenses change its recovery by less than 0.03 F1. Since no sensitive content is ever transmitted, content-level inspection cannot detect this leakage; we therefore propose Selection-Obfuscated Retrieval (SOR), which acquires resources under a fixed budget independent of private context and confines the agent's selection to a local snapshot, cutting SNOOP's F1 to 0.39 without additional LLM inference.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Partially_canonical_axes_in_large_language_model_representations|Partially canonical axes in large language model representations]]

![[assets/2611.26920_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26920)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26920
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Partially_canonical_axes_in_large_language_model_representations|Partially canonical axes in large language model representations]]
- **作者**: Andrea Gregor de Varda, Ferdinando D'Elia, Greta Tuckute, Evelina Fedorenko, Meenakshi Khosla
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 0.85（加权：大模型 0.85）
- **关联关键词**: LLM

#### 研究背景与动机

《Partially canonical axes in large language model representations》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) develop rich internal representations of language, but whether their individual coordinate axes carry meaning, or whether meaning is encoded only in directions in representation space, is still an open question. A common assumption is that individual axes in LLMs are arbitrary. We here challenge this view: across 12 LLMs from six model families, the native axes are reliably more aligned with other models' representations, with human brain responses to language, and with interpretable linguistic features than randomly rotated axes that preserve the same geometry. The native axes nonetheless fall well short of the best achievable alignment, so LLM coordinates are not fully arbitrary but also not fully canonical: they are partially canonical. A theory of how LLMs represent language must explain both the population geometry they learn and why individual units acquire patterns of response profiles that recur across systems.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/AstronautBench_Measuring_Agent_Self-Improvement_Across_Episodes_Through_Knowledge,_Evidence,_and_Progress|AstronautBench: Measuring Agent Self-Improvement Across Episodes Through Knowledge, Evidence, and Progress]]

![[assets/2611.26910_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26910)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26910
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/AstronautBench_Measuring_Agent_Self-Improvement_Across_Episodes_Through_Knowledge,_Evidence,_and_Progress|AstronautBench: Measuring Agent Self-Improvement Across Episodes Through Knowledge, Evidence, and Progress]]
- **作者**: Congyuan Yu, Runping Tian
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.05（加权：具身智能 0.3，大模型 0.75）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《AstronautBench: Measuring Agent Self-Improvement Across Episodes Through Knowledge, Evidence, and Progress》归入 大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ASIWorld, AbstainEQA, AdaExploreBench, AstronautBench, DiscoveryWorld, EQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM agents are expected to improve themselves from their own experience across episodes, with fixed weights. Self-improvement in an embodied world cannot be read from one measure. Existing benchmarks measure task progress, world-knowledge question answering (QA), and episodic memory, but a correct answer does not show that the agent ever observed the evidence for it, and progress does not show what the agent learned. We introduce AstronautBench, which measures one agent's self-improvement through three views on the same run: what it knows, whether its own evidence supports that knowledge, and how far it gets in the world. It is built on the game Outer Wilds, where each episode is a 22-minute loop after which the world resets, only the agent's knowledge carries over, and progress is gated by knowledge. After every episode, we measure the places reached and ship-log rumor facts revealed, the correctness of answers to 164 questions about the world, and whether each answer is supported by the frames and position the agent cites from its own recorded play. Two LLM judges from different model families check every answer. In eight-episode baselines with GPT-6.1 Sol and Gemini 4 Argon planners, which act through a learned controller or their own gamepad inputs, the three views do not move together, so any one of them alone gives an incomplete picture of whether the agent is improving. The GPT-6.1 Sol agent's correct answers grow from 14.6% to 26.8% of all questions, but at episode 8 only 59 to 68% of them are supported by its cited evidence. It reveals no new rumor facts after episode 3, while the places it reaches and its memory keep growing. More than half of the answers that become correct after episode 3 cite only evidence from earlier episodes.

</details>

- **作者**: Qiang Zhou, Jianing Deng, Siyan Dong, Jingxian Chen, Han-Yi Lin, Jingtong Hu
