#### 研究背景与动机

《Perceive Once, Escalate on Demand: Handing Off Visual KV Memory from Small to Large VLMs》归入 大模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenEQA, VSI-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

An embodied agent cannot afford a large vision-language model (VLM) at every step of a long video, so a cascade runs a small VLM continuously and escalates only uncertain decisions to a large one. Each escalation, however, forces the large model to re-encode a history the small model has already perceived, and this dominates its cost. We present Baton, which hands the large model the small model's visual memory, its key-value (KV) cache over the history. Baton rests on one finding: the large model needs a usable memory, not a faithful copy of its own. A map trained to reproduce the large model's cache matches it most closely, yet brings no gain over the small model alone. Baton instead distills a per-layer linear map, without labels, from the large model's own answers, with the large model encoding only the current frame. Interventions on the map trace the gain to one direction, carried by the middle third of the large model's layers. On RoboMME, Baton beats the small model alone, a pruned or shortened re-watch, and a text memory; as the escalation channel of a cascade it matches re-watching to within about one point at 28-47% less compute, and 8x less per escalation at 64 frames. Its gain grows with the large model, from 8B to 32B, tracks the large model's headroom across eight model pairs from four families, and transfers zero-shot to OpenEQA.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ProbeAgent_Principled_Diagnosis_of_Agent_Failures_in_Verifiable_Environments|ProbeAgent: Principled Diagnosis of Agent Failures in Verifiable Environments]]

![[assets/2611.26897_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26897)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26897
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ProbeAgent_Principled_Diagnosis_of_Agent_Failures_in_Verifiable_Environments|ProbeAgent: Principled Diagnosis of Agent Failures in Verifiable Environments]]
- **作者**: Yue Huang, Yuchen Ma, Shuhang Lin, Yuyang Wu, Zixuan Weng, Zixiang Xu, Xiangqi Wang, Yuexing Hao, Yujun Zhou, Yue Zhao, Olexandr Isayev, Stefan Feuerriegel...
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 0.95（加权：大模型 0.95）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent

#### 研究背景与动机

《ProbeAgent: Principled Diagnosis of Agent Failures in Verifiable Environments》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Improving agents requires discovering where they fail and understanding why. An agent acts over multiple steps in a changing environment, so diagnosing its failures requires more than inspecting the final response of a large language model (LLM). The same unsuccessful outcome can arise from task difficulty or from an error the agent repeats across tasks. We introduce ProbeAgent, an automated framework that discovers agent failures, tests their proposed causes, and identifies shared failure modes. Starting from benchmark tasks, ProbeAgent builds executable tests and validates them using reference solutions, automated checks, and review of the task requirements. For each failed trajectory, it explains the error and constructs new tasks that preserve the suspected cause while changing the surrounding context. Running the agent on these tasks tests whether the same error recurs. ProbeAgent then groups failures with similar causes and generates descriptions that distinguish their errors. This process connects explanations of individual failures to executable tests and descriptions of shared failure modes. We evaluate ProbeAgent across 22 agent configurations using tasks from 10 benchmarks, collecting 1,244 failures and producing 536 failure-mode descriptions. These descriptions distinguish specific errors within broad outcomes such as incomplete work, including planning without acting and stopping before delivering a file. In human evaluation, annotators accept 90.0% of assigned descriptions and correctly reject 83.3% of similar alternatives, supporting the accuracy and specificity of the discovered failure modes.

</details>

![[assets/2611.26881_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26881](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26881)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26881
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Your_Judge_Inherits_Your_Prompt_In-Context_Label_Bias_in_an_LLM-as-Judge_Is_Directional,_Not_Symmetric|Your Judge Inherits Your Prompt: In-Context Label Bias in an LLM-as-Judge Is Directional, Not Symmetric]]
- **作者**: Abdullah Ahmad, Ahmed J. O. Ahmed, Luca Citi
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 0.85（加权：大模型 0.85）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Multimodal, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《Your Judge Inherits Your Prompt: In-Context Label Bias in an LLM-as-Judge Is Directional, Not Symmetric》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MSVD-QA, NExT-QA, VideoQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM-as-judge has become a standard way to score open-ended model outputs, yet the judge is itself a frozen language model steered by a few in-context examples, and such models are known to absorb the label balance of their demonstrations rather than treat them as neutral illustrations. We ask whether this label bias reaches an LLM judge used inside a multimodal (video question answering) evaluation pipeline, and what form it takes. Using a fully frozen generator-judge pipeline, we hold the generated answers fixed and vary only the Yes/No balance of the judge's few-shot examples across four demonstration sets. Each verdict is validated against a two-annotator human consensus on 200 examples per dataset. The judge's acceptance rate stays close to human consensus when its examples are balanced or skewed toward rejection, but jumps sharply (by about 30 points on NExT-QA and 20 on MSVD-QA, with a strongly one-sided error profile) when the examples are skewed toward acceptance; the opposite skew produces no matching drop. The bias is therefore one-directional within the tested range of label ratios. A separate five-draw analysis shows that results from a single example draw are unreliable, varying by up to 18 points, more than any difference between prompting conditions. We conclude that demonstration label bias in an LLM judge is a directional, inflation-only failure mode that can fabricate apparent benchmark gains but not losses, and that it is cheaply avoided by balancing the judge's examples and reporting results averaged over several draws.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Molecular dynamics (MD) simulation resolves atomic-scale motion and predicts the structural, mechanical, and dynamic properties of materials, making it cen- tral to materials design and discovery. Yet an MD study requires an expert-driven workflow: designing the simulation, writing reliable input scripts, executing them, and analyzing the results with separate tools. Here we present ChatAtom, a multi-agent large language model system that translates a natural-language request into a complete MD workflow without the user writing input scripts or analysis code. Each agent has a defined responsibility within the workflow. An orchestrator plans the study; the LAMMPS and OVITO agents each use a generator-reviewer pair to prepare and review simulation and visualization scripts before execution; a high-performance computing agent dispatches jobs to remote resources; and an analysis agent interprets the completed run. The work- flow is accessible through a browser interface that streams thermodynamic data and rendered frames during local execution, allowing users to follow the simu- lation as it progresses. The architecture also supports new capabilities through loadable skills without modifying the agents, enabling autonomous hypothesis generation and testing. We evaluate ChatAtom through script-generation bench- marks, an end-to-end study of crack propagation, and a closed-loop campaign. By bringing script generation, execution, visualization, and analysis into one reviewed and observable pipeline, ChatAtom lowers the barrier to conducting MD studies and broadens access to advanced simulation workflows.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《What Helps Language-Model Agents Review Violent Video? A Controlled Comparison with a Human Evidence Reference》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AudioSet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Systems that flag violent video for a human operator can now be built from language models without training, by combining perception models and letting a language model decide. We ask how the observations of the perception models should reach the decision, and which evidence matters. On the XD-Violence test set (799 clips) we fix one set of perception agents (video description, object detection, sound tagging, speech recognition and speech tagging) and one reasoning model, and compare three interfaces: messages between agents, a typed evidence graph explored with tools, and flat text read in one pass; and a single audio-visual model given the raw clip. Two annotators provide a human reference that records the decision, the incident segments and whether they relied on sight, sound or speech. Flat text matches the single model (F1 0.967 against 0.968), while the graph agent and message passing are less accurate; a variant of the graph agent developed on a separate set did not close the gap, and both graph summaries and multi-turn tool use lowered accuracy. These results hold with reworded prompts and with a larger reasoning model, except that the larger model weakens message passing enough for the graph agent to beat it. Sound events improve F1 by +0.012 [+0.004, +0.021], in line with the annotators, who used sound or speech on most violent clips. Citations of stored observations are faithful, changing about half of the decisions when removed, but no more so with the graph than with flat text, and the agent pipeline costs about 3 times the GPU time of the single model. We also report two artefacts of vision-language describers that mislead pipelines built on them.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Specification-guided reinforcement learning (RL) translates formal specifications of desired behavior of agents into shaped rewards. This paper introduces SLICR, a significant paradigm shift that advances the state of the art to specification-guided RL in adversarial settings under partial observability. We employ Strategy Logic (SL), which allows explicit quantification over agents' strategies and uses the linear temporal logic (LTL) to specify the agents' objectives and constraints. This enables reasoning not only about how agents should act to satisfy a specification, but also about how they should respond to the strategies and behaviors of other agents. We evaluate SLICR on three popular benchmarks: Predator-Prey, Google Research Football, and our home-grown extension of SMAC-HARD, demonstrating its effectiveness, scalability, and ability to adapt to diverse adversarial strategies.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Learning_Inside_Types_Epistemic_Typestates_for_Safe_Multi-Agent_Reinforcement_Learning|Learning Inside Types: Epistemic Typestates for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning]]

![[assets/2611.27631_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27631)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27631
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Learning_Inside_Types_Epistemic_Typestates_for_Safe_Multi-Agent_Reinforcement_Learning|Learning Inside Types: Epistemic Typestates for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning]]
- **作者**: Lorenzo Bacchiani, Mario Bravetti, Giovanni Pau, Roberto Girau
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.3（加权：大模型 0.5，强化学习 0.8）

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning Inside Types: Epistemic Typestates for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Type disciplines make programs safe by construction: components checked against each other's types compose into programs that cannot go wrong in the ways the types exclude. Types suit reinforcement learning: a type constrains what an agent may choose, not how it chooses, so the guarantee holds for every policy a learner produces. We introduce epistemic typestates, a type discipline for safe multi-agent reinforcement learning. An agent's type is a protocol whose states record its beliefs, the worlds it cannot rule out from its own observations and actions, and permits only actions that are safe in every world of the belief, whatever the others do within their public types. Agents learn inside their types, which are built and checked one agent at a time and run without communication. We prove type soundness and team safety, give a synthesis that is sound and complete, build types from guards when worlds are infinite, and show that shields are a special case. All proofs are mechanised in Rocq. On nine benchmarks, no typed learner causes a safety violation, and task performance does not differ significantly from that of two recent multi-agent shielding methods. Where a contract-based method fails at five and ten agents, types for ten agents are built in under a second. In the two continuous benchmarks, Lagrangian learners cause thousands of violations in every training run.

</details>

---

#### 研究背景与动机

《Sketch2TikZ: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning for Converting Hand-Drawn Sketches to Executable TikZ》归入 强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CodeRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Automatic digitization of hand-drawn sketches into editable formats is crucial for modern document workflows and LaTeX integration. Converting sketches into executable TikZ code provides a structured representation of diagrams, but remains challenging due to noisy and ambiguous inputs, which hinder reliable interpretation and limit the effectiveness of token-level supervision for ensuring global diagram validity. To address these challenges, we propose Sketch2TikZ, a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes sketch-to-code generation via a sequential multi-reward strategy, combining Code-Structure ($\mathcal{R}_{cs}$), OCR ($\mathcal{R}_{ocr}$), CleanDIFT ($\mathcal{R}_{cd}$), and Self-Similarity ($\mathcal{R}_{ss}$) rewards to enforce textual, visual and structural correctness. We evaluate Sketch2TikZ on the SkeTikZ and FlowChart-3B benchmarks using metrics including compilation success rate (CSR), image similarity, code similarity, and character similarity. Our method achieves near-perfect compilation success and improves visual and textual alignment over strong open-source baselines, while remaining competitive with proprietary GPT-based vision-language models. Extensive ablations demonstrate that curriculum-based reward scheduling is critical for reducing reward interference, stabilizing training and mitigating proxy over-optimization. Overall, our results highlight the effectiveness of reference-aware, multi-objective reinforcement learning for structured diagram generation from noisy sketches.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《WEIR: Watershed Exploration and Drift-Aware Prefix Shrinkage for Multi-Turn Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ALFWorld, CoBA-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multi-turn language model agents often receive only a single binary reward at the end of a long interaction trajectory, making it difficult to separate decisive decisions from routine steps or estimate accurate intermediate values. Prefix-tree methods improve sample efficiency by merging trajectories that share action histories, yet two structural flaws remain: naive pooling mixes stale off-policy outcomes into critic targets with fixed weights, and uniform or entropy-driven branching wastes continuations on dead-end states whose outcome is already settled. We introduce WEIR (Watershed Exploration and Intrinsic-Drift Shrinkage), an actor-critic reinforcement learning framework grounded in a Doob turn-variance conservation identity. WEIR maintains a cross-iteration canonical action-prefix tree with exponentially discounted visit counts and trains a prefix-conditioned critic toward a minimax drift-aware shrinkage target that adapts from single-trajectory returns at rare nodes to pooled empirical means at dense nodes. Using the same pre-update critic, WEIR allocates rollout budgets across tasks via Neyman stratification $B_q \propto \sqrt{\hat{p}_q(1-\hat{p}_q)}$ and directs within-task branches to watershed forks where high survival-headroom uncertainty coincides with steep value cliffs. Across four multi-hop QA benchmarks, ALFWorld, and WebShop on Qwen2.5-3B/7B-Instruct, WEIR achieves $32.18\%$ and $37.85\%$ average QA exact match and $84.83\%$ and $87.81\%$ ALFWorld success rates, outperforming CAPO by $1.50$--$3.74$ points while generating $28$--$29\%$ fewer rollout tokens than chain sampling.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《TITAN: DRL-Based Dynamic Scheduling and Resource Allocation for Tactile Internet》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Tactile Internet (TI) applications require ultra-reliable, low-latency tactile communication alongside video traffic. However, tactile Quality of Service (QoS) requirements vary with the interaction context, making fixed resource allocation inefficient. In this paper, we propose TITAN (Two-Timescale Integrated Tactile Internet over 5G Access Networks), a dynamic scheduling and resource allocation (SRA) scheme. TITAN receives time-varying tactile QoS profiles from a higher-layer module and adapts SRA to the current QoS profile and network state. A deep reinforcement learning-based agent splits the available network bandwidth between tactile and video streams at a slow timescale, while a fast-timescale scheduler allocates resource blocks among active user equipment. Through simulations, we show that TITAN simultaneously satisfies both tactile and video QoS targets and achieves a performance gain of 7.64 percentage points (pp) in the joint satisfaction rate over the best-performing static SRA baseline.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Learning_When_to_Ask_Advisor-Aware_Post-Training_for_SLM_Agents|Learning When to Ask: Advisor-Aware Post-Training for SLM Agents]]

![[assets/2611.27443_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27443](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27443)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27443
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Learning_When_to_Ask_Advisor-Aware_Post-Training_for_SLM_Agents|Learning When to Ask: Advisor-Aware Post-Training for SLM Agents]]
- **作者**: Aasheesh Singh, Vishal Vaddina
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG

- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 大模型, 具身智能, 世界模型

- **关联关键词**: Agent, EmbodiedAI, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning When to Ask: Advisor-Aware Post-Training for SLM Agents》归入 强化学习、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LiveCodeBench, TextWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly attractive for cheap, high-throughput agentic deployment, but they remain weak at multi-step reasoning and tool use that long-horizon tasks demand. We study how to post-train an SLM to seek advice from a stronger model and act on that advice itself, following an advisor-executor pattern. Out of the box, this pattern fails across open- and closed-source small-model families: wiring an SLM to an advisor is a harness-design matter that is not enough on its own. A post-training recipe is needed to teach the model when to ask, in a cost-aware fashion, and how to follow advice across steps. Our method, ADAPT (ADvisor-Aware Post-Training), is a multi-stage recipe that instills this behavior, learning entirely from the policy's own rollouts supervised by the environment's outcome, with no learned reward model or human annotation. Across three domains, compared to an untrained harness baseline, ADAPT improves success rate in embodied text reasoning (TextWorld) from 24.5% to 77.3%, code generation (LiveCodeBench) from 21.8% to 56.0%, and deep web search (GAIA) from 17.5% to 49.3%, while querying the advisor sparingly (~1.0-2.1 calls per task). On the two long-horizon domains it closes 70-85% of the gap to an oracle that perfectly times when to ask for advice, and on code generation it exceeds that oracle. The gains persist when the advisor is replaced by one unseen in training, showing that the learned help-seeking policy is robust to a changing advisor rather than tied to the one it was trained with.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Testing_for_Coordination_Failures_in_Cooperative_Multi-Agent_Reinforcement_Learning|Testing for Coordination Failures in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning]]

![[assets/2611.27368_first_page.png|800]]

- **作者**: Dennis Gross, Jørn Eirik Betten, Helge Spieker
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.SE
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Testing for Coordination Failures in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CMARL, MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Deep parallel cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (CMARL)} trains multiple agents that all act at every time step to optimize a common objective. Like single-agent RL, CMARL can produce unsafe behaviors that violate implicit safety requirements not captured by the reward, and these behaviors are hard to explain. However, testing CMARL policies for safety has received less attention than the safety testing of single-agent RL policies. In this paper, we propose DisQ, a test case selection method that identifies failure-revealing states in value-based CMARL by exploiting a signal specific to trained Q-learning agents. The signal is the Q-value gap, an explainable metric that flags a state when the agents' estimates of the achievable return diverge by more than a calibrated threshold. DisQ selects such states for execution and skips those without a gap. Our experiments in four CMARL benchmark environments show that, in most of the evaluated configurations, DisQ improves failure detection over single-agent RL testing methods applied to CMARL, namely random testing and search-based testing with genetic algorithms. Our main contributions are the Q-value gap, an indicator of potential miscoordination computable from a single state without executing the system, and DisQ, a test case selection method that adapts existing single-agent testing methods to CMARL.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27148_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27148)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27148
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Structure_Over_Scale_Rethinking_Adaptation_for_Reinforcement_Learning_with_Verifiable_Rewards|Structure Over Scale: Rethinking Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards]]
- **作者**: Allan Kazakov, Abdurrahman Javat
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.12（加权：强化学习 0.96，世界模型 0.16）
- **关联关键词**: RL, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《Structure Over Scale: Rethinking Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Base-to-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is often framed as a choice between the expressivity of Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) and the efficiency of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). We find that this framing misses an important variable: the geometry of the update. Under a common cold-start GRPO protocol, LoRA exhibits rank collapse: performance remains broadly stable at low rank, then fails abruptly as rank increases. On DeepMath-Hard, for example, accuracy drops from 73.1% at r=64 to 4.7% at r=128. In contrast, Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) and Quantum-Informed Tensor Adaptation (QuanTA) remain stable over a wider tested optimization region and are competitive with FFT while updating less than 0.6% of the model parameters. Spectral analysis reveals a corresponding difference in learned update structure: collapsed LoRA concentrates its update energy into relatively few effective directions, whereas structured adapters produce substantially more distributed updates. These results suggest that nominal rank and parameter count alone are insufficient to characterize adaptation in RLVR - the structure imposed on the reachable update manifold also matters. Finally, across three 8B base models, adapter behavior aligns with both frozen-weight spectral structure and reported pre-training scale, motivating the Model Maturity Hypothesis: the adaptation geometry that works best may depend on properties already encoded during pre-training. We present this as a falsifiable hypothesis rather than a causal conclusion. The code can be found here https://github.com/Shrimpstanot/structure-over-scale

</details>

- **作者**: Ruijia Yang, Ruikai Li, Zeyi Wen
- **cs 子类**: cs.DC, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 大模型, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.42（加权：大模型 0.3，强化学习 0.96，世界模型 0.16）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《CTRL: Concurrent Training and Rollout for LLM Reinforcement Learning via GPU Co-Residency》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CTRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models runs two GPU workloads with different hardware demands: rollout decoding, which is dominated by memory access and whose batch shrinks as responses finish, and a dense learner. Current systems either time-share every GPU between them, paying serial phases and switches, or split GPUs into pools whose sizes change only in whole devices. We present CTRL, which makes co-residency a third RL execution layout: every GPU runs a learner rank beside a persistent rollout engine, sharing the device under CUDA MPS. A host-owned learner makes the layout fit: optimizer state stays in host memory, and policy weights stream layer by layer through a bounded GPU window. A publication gate trains each ready batch first and waits only before releasing the next policy, keeping a dropless staleness bound. A step-time bound guides compute-share selection, and a GPU-time ledger explains overlap and sharing costs. On H100, A800, and RTX 4090 GPUs with Qwen3 models, CTRL achieves 1.11-2.24$\times$ the throughput of the stronger of verl's asynchronous baselines across 24 workload settings with the staleness limit set to two, and 1.23$\times$ verl's colocate_async on eight H100 GPUs. With the same learner, co-residency is 1.10-1.26$\times$ faster than time-sharing on all three platforms and 1.10-1.30$\times$ faster than the best measured whole-GPU split on H100 DAPO-Math workloads at 256-528 trajectories per step, on four and eight GPUs.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Reward Integrity in Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for LLM Guardrail Red-Teaming》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AdvPIIBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We study reward fidelity in an adversarial reinforcement-learning loop for red-teaming financial-services language-model guardrails. Apparent wins can arise when an attack omits prescribed personally identifiable information (PII), or when a reward judge credits a response that did not break the targeted policy. On attacker-generated and public benchmark examples, TypeSafe Jev checks whether the prescribed PII is present. For outcome scoring, we compare the decision models TypeSafe Jev, Laya (pretrained and post-trained), and pretrained CLM-8B with logged Opus 4.8 judgements from a replay corpus. Post-training markedly improves Laya's agreement with the judge. A separate author-reviewed off-target set establishes that Opus can award false wins. The findings motivate specialised decision-model judges for checking prescribed PII in attack inputs and scoring target responses against the intended policy.

</details>

![[assets/2611.26909_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26909)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26909
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/AI-Assisted_Decision_Support_for_Formula_1_Pit-Stop_Strategy_Deep_Reinforcement_Learning_and_Mathematical_Programming|AI-Assisted Decision Support for Formula 1 Pit-Stop Strategy: Deep Reinforcement Learning and Mathematical Programming]]
- **作者**: Felipe Méndez, Charles Thraves
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.9（加权：大模型 0.1，强化学习 1.8）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《AI-Assisted Decision Support for Formula 1 Pit-Stop Strategy: Deep Reinforcement Learning and Mathematical Programming》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Pit-stop strategy in Formula 1 is a sequential decision problem under uncertainty, in which strategists decide at each lap whether to pit and which tire compound to fit. We develop a data-driven simulator, calibrated with historical race data, that captures tire degradation, drivers' recent performance, overtaking, yellow flag events, and rivals' pit-stop decisions. We train agents with five Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) algorithms and compare them with a Quadratic Mixed-Integer Programming (QMIP) formulation that is optimal in deterministic single-driver settings. Without uncertainty or competition, all DRL methods except TRPO achieve near-optimal performance. With a full grid of competitors and stochastic race events, the ranking of DRL methods depends on the evaluation setting: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and its recurrent variant are the only methods that improve on the QMIP both in fully simulated races and when the actual 2024 races are replayed. Averaged over eight circuits, PPO improves on the QMIP by 0.33 and 0.49 positions in the two settings and Recurrent PPO by less, and in the replay PPO finishes 0.66 positions ahead of the drivers' actual strategies simulated in the same races. The QMIP performs similarly to the other methods, even though it cannot anticipate yellow flag events. Finally, we use the trained PPO policy as a look-ahead decision-support tool that shows the strategist the distribution of race outcomes under each candidate pit-stop action. Applied retrospectively to eight 2024 races, the look-ahead identifies the laps where the actual decision, the recommendation, or a third action was preferable.

</details>

《Reinforcement Learning Unlearns Corrupted SFT Reasoning》归入 强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：SFT-then-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We show that reinforcement learning (RL) in reasoning tasks can succeed even when the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data is severely corrupted. In a mathematical reasoning dataset (GSM8K), we systematically corrupt training trajectories across 361 configurations by injecting realistic error types, varying error locations, trace-answer consistency, and corruption ratios up to 100%. While aggregated SFT-only performance steadily deteriorates under corruption (dropping from 43.61% to 13.80%), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) post-training consistently restores model accuracy back to gold-baseline levels (approximately 60%). Crucially, initializing RL with as few as N = 8 minimal, incorrect, three-line "form-only" traces boosts post-RL performance to 69.30%, outperforming standard gold SFT initialization (60.71%) and GRPO without SFT (35.00%). Further, we show that SFT accuracy is largely uncorrelated with post-RL accuracy (r approximately 0), demonstrating a fundamental asymmetry in post-training: SFT primarily establishes structural layout (form), while RL independently discovers functional reasoning (function).

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Grid_Cells_as_Spectral_Successor_Features_A_Reusable_Basis_for_Continual_Reinforcement_Learning|Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning]]

![[assets/2611.26504_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26504](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26504)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26504
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Grid_Cells_as_Spectral_Successor_Features_A_Reusable_Basis_for_Continual_Reinforcement_Learning|Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning]]
- **作者**: Élodie Côté-Gauthier, Doina Precup, Isabeau Prémont-Schwarz
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.NE
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 大模型, 世界模型

- **关联关键词**: Agent, RL, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《Grid Cells as Spectral Successor Features: A Reusable Basis for Continual Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Continual reinforcement learning requires agents to adapt when the task changes while reusing what they know about the environment. Successor features separate dynamics from task-specific reward, but their effectiveness depends on the choice of base features. Taking the identification of entorhinal grid cells with eigenvectors of the successor representation as a design principle, we build this basis from eigenvectors of the transition matrix of a fixed reference policy. With such a basis, the successor features at a state-action pair are a linear combination of the base features at that same pair, which is not true in general, and the coefficients are known in closed form: each successor feature is its base feature multiplied by $θ_i = 1/(1-γλ_i)$, where $λ_i$ is its eigenvalue. The long-term return is thus predicted from the same features as the immediate reward, no successor-feature network or replay buffer is needed, and adapting to a new task reduces to a linear regression on the reward. The agent acts greedily on the action values of the reference policy, which is one step of policy improvement over it and suffices to reach a single goal. In a two-goal gridworld, with the uniform random walk as reference policy, whose eigenvectors are the low-frequency Laplacian eigenvectors, agents built this way maintain near-maximal return across every change of goal, whereas SimpleSF and DQN lose most of their return at each change.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LingBot-World, NoPeBench, NoPeWorld, WBench, WorldRoamBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We study a new problem of preventing object penetration in interactive video world models. To understand how existing models handle this, we introduce NoPeBench, a benchmark that evaluates how world models respond to physical obstacles. Our evaluation reveals that none of the open-source models can reliably prevent such failures. Surprisingly, however, these models already encode the information needed to predict such violations. We find that the hidden states of diffusion transformers (DiTs) can predict whether a candidate action will penetrate an obstacle. Building on this, we introduce NoPeWorld, a lightweight framework that detects and prevents penetration. Specifically, we train a small prediction head on top of a frozen DiT's hidden states to read out if a candidate action is safe or not. Unsafe actions are then replaced with safe ones that best preserve the original intent. Despite its simplicity, NoPeWorld substantially reduces the physical violation rate, averaged over six video world models, from 74% to 16%. Remarkably, our method shows zero-shot generalization to open-domain imagery, third-person viewpoints, and moving obstacles. We publicly release our code, the NoPeBench benchmark, and model weights. Project Page: https://nopeworld.github.io .

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond Visual Fidelity: Comparing Categorical and Continuous World Models》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：VBench, World-in-World, WorldModelBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visual world models can now generate compelling interactive worlds, but visual realism does not guarantee faithful dynamics. We systematically compare how categorical and continuous prediction shape learned dynamics under both next-token and iterative whole-frame generation. These choices are often bundled in approaches such as autoregressive token models and whole-frame flow matching. We design controlled 2D and 3D environments and evaluate dynamics validity, calibration, and visual quality. In 2D with lossless visual encoding, categorical models achieve higher rollout validity with less training compute, particularly for stochastic entities. Among categorical models, greedy decoding produces the highest rollout validity, while sampling from a next-token model yields the best-calibrated stochastic predictions. Failures in continuous models, such as disappearing game characters, echo those seen in more complex environments. Here, known dynamics let us measure their impact. In 3D, categorical models also achieve stronger long-horizon performance with less training compute. Even models with lossy tokenization remain competitive on semantic accuracy and FVD, despite lower next-frame RGB accuracy. Together, these findings make a strong case for categorical prediction as a compute-efficient route to learning world dynamics.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《SOAR: Training-Free Rank-Consistent Rollout Compression for Latent World Model Planning》归入 世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MetaWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Latent world models plan by rolling out hundreds of candidate action sequences through a transformer predictor, so a single decision can take tens of seconds. Existing accelerations prune spatial tokens as in vision-language models, yet a sampling-based planner uses predicted costs only to rank candidates, so a rollout can be compressed freely as long as the ranking survives. We propose SOAR, a training-free method that adapts rollout compression to each planning decision. At every decision, SOAR probes a few candidates under several token budgets and keeps the smallest budget that preserves their ranking, so it compresses as far as the current decision allows, without task labels. A closed-loop study of more than 80,000 episodes fixes the rest of the rollout: the first step, which alone reads a real observation, stays exact, and the context frame is cached rather than recomputed. On seven navigation and manipulation tasks with frozen checkpoints, SOAR matches dense-level planning success at 2.3x speedup and retains 98% at 2.5x. Our code is available at https://github.com/starrywiki/SOAR.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond Future Views: Exploring Future-State World Models for Vision-Language Navigation》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language Navigation(VLN) requires an embodied agent to follow language instructions through unseen 3D environments, where the target spaces often lie beyond the current observation. Navigation world models address this challenge by predicting future views either as pixels or latent representations, providing valuable look-ahead information for action decision. Both paradigms define the future primarily through what the agent may observe, raising a fundamental question: is predicting future views the ultimate form to represent the future for VLN? Cognitive studies show that the human hippocampus prospectively represents both future goals and intervening locations along planned routes. Inspired by this predictive-map view, we organize the navigation-relevant future around goal-directed spatial states and route structure. We instantiate this perspective with two complementary abstractions: a short-horizon waypoint that specifies the next local state to reach, and a mid-horizon corridor that captures the spatial support of the upcoming route. Based on this design, we propose FS-Nav, a future-state world modeling framework that predicts waypoint-corridor states from spatial memory and language instructions, and communicates them to a VLM navigator through top-down and first-person interfaces. Experiments on standard VLN benchmarks demonstrate that FS-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance. This highlights navigation-relevant future-state modeling as a promising direction for representing the future in VLN.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/PhysForce_Physics-aware_Force_Control_for_Interactive_Video_World_Model|PhysForce: Physics-aware Force Control for Interactive Video World Model]]

![[assets/2611.27164_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《PhysForce: Physics-aware Force Control for Interactive Video World Model》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：PARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Interactive video world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve in response to user interventions, including physically consistent state changes induced by applied forces. Existing methods often represent forces as motion trajectories, which may introduce inappropriate motion priors, causing the model to over-rely on encoded displacement patterns. More fundamentally, the model's internal representations do not reliably localize force-induced state changes, making it difficult to associate applied forces with their resulting physical responses. Also, limited force-annotated data make it difficult to produce physically consistent and temporally stable predictions in unseen scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose PhysForce, a two-stage framework that integrates supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning through three complementary components. During supervised fine-tuning, (1) the Latent Force Intervention Encoder maps force location, direction, and magnitude into the video latent space without exposing future motion; and (2) the Physics Attribution Network associates force interventions with localized changes in the latent state, strengthening the learning of physical responses to applied forces. During reinforcement learning, (3) Physics-Aware Reinforcement Learning uses self-supervised rewards on out-of-distribution videos to improve physical consistency and generalization. Extensive experiments show that PhysForce improves force responsiveness, physical consistency, and generalization, demonstrating its effectiveness for interactive video world models.

</details>

- **关联关键词**: Agent, WorldModel, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《A Plan or an Adversarial Example? A Round-Trip Check for Latent World Models》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ImageNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

If we optimize an image until a classifier is confident it shows a cat, we may get an adversarial example, not a cat. Planning with a latent world model is a similar search for actions whose predicted future is closest to the goal. Is the result a plan, or an adversarial example of the world model? We show that it can be either. On the released DINO-WM and LeWM, more iterations of the cross-entropy method (CEM) lower the predicted cost but lose up to half of the problems CEM had already solved. The model's own predictions do not reveal these failures. We propose a simple test that executes the plan after every iteration and counts the problems a planner solves and then loses. Unlike an image, a plan comes with its actions, which our round-trip check reads back from the predicted states with a small network, penalizing mismatches. The check reads back the sum of each block of five actions, the part that two consecutive predicted states determine. Without retraining the world model and at only 0.2% to 3.1% more planning time, it improves the widely used planners CEM and MPPI on three released world models, and gains more where planners lose more problems. Without replanning, success rises from 0.39 to 0.76 on Wall with DINO-WM and from 0.56 to 0.94 for MPPI on JEPA-WM. With replanning (MPC), it rises from 0.70 to 0.81 on Cube with LeWM.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27029_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Weikang Qiu, Zheng Huang, Tinglin Huang, Yujun Yan, Rex Ying
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG

- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 具身智能, 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Mind World Models: Joint Learning of Motor EEG Representations and Action-Conditioned World Dynamics》归入 世界模型、具身智能、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Motor decoding from electroencephalography (EEG) supports non-invasive brain-computer interfaces, yet existing methods either rely on costly cue-based trials and coarse categorical labels or use pretraining objectives that do not directly capture how movement unfolds, limiting their ability to learn transferable motor representations. To this end, we propose the Mind World Model (MWM), a framework that learns motor EEG representations from scalable naturalistic EEG and egocentric video without action annotations. MWM treats EEG observed up to the current frame as the latent action of a world model: a mind model encodes the EEG, and a world model uses it to predict the representation of a future frame, yielding a movement-grounded pretraining objective. We further introduce the Lateralization-Aware Transformer (LAT), which uses frame averaging to separate shared bilateral from side-specific lateral features, encoding hemispheric correspondence to reduce the burden of learning left-right relationships from data. MWM pretraining produces two models: a mind model for downstream motor decoding and a world model that can be adapted to prediction conditioned on real actions, opening a scalable and flexible path to collect egocentric data for embodied AI. Across 9 classification and 7 regression tasks, the mind model improves task-averaged balanced accuracy by 5.9% and Pearson correlation by 16.2% relative to the strongest baseline in each task family. Compared with an inverse-dynamics latent-action baseline, the world model achieves lower prediction error and benefits 3.3 times more from matched real actions within recordings and 4.7 times more on held-out subjects.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《ReactDrive: Self-Reactive Driving World Model》归入 世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The world evolves through the dynamics of its constituent entities and their interactions, with changes in one entity influencing the behavior of others. To simulate this evolution under different actions, interactive world models need to capture the resulting agent responses as part of the world's own dynamics. In autonomous driving, errors in modeling these responses can misrepresent maneuver safety and undermine the reliability of generated videos used for policy evaluation and learning. To formalize this intrinsic requirement for driving world model, we define self-reactivity as the capability to model agent responses from observed history and a specified ego action, and generate visual futures that reflect those responses without prescribed future agent trajectories. To realize this capability, we introduce ReactDrive, a driving world model that jointly learns agent dynamics and visual generation through a shared agent state representation. Within this framework, dynamics supervision grounds a reactive dynamics prior in observed agent states, while the video objective jointly optimizes the self-reactivity and visual realism. Experiments show improved agent responses, ego controllability, and video quality, a 96.0% preference for agent reactivity in human comparisons across five baselines, and better downstream planning through augmentation with generated videos. We hope this work encourages the community to recognize self-reactivityas a foundational capability for developing and evaluating interactive world models. Project page: https://reactdrive.github.io/ .

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《ViewDreamer: A Robot World Model beyond Fixed Camera Setups》归入 世界模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AgiBot-World, Ctrl-World, GE-Sim, MultiWorld, PAIWorld, RoboView-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Video generation models are increasingly important for robot learning, yet most generate each camera view separately or tile all views in a fixed layout. Robot demonstrations, however, are captured by synchronized cameras whose number, placement, and order vary across embodiments. We present ViewDreamer, a single world model that handles variable sets of cameras by disentangling per-view generation from cross-view interaction. Each view follows the generation path of a pretrained single-view model, and views interact only through shared slot tokens. We stabilize multi-view training with a curriculum that gradually increases the number of views. Cross-View Slot Agreement uses visual foundation models to group geometrically and semantically similar regions across views into shared slots. On RoboView-Bench, which covers one to four cameras, ViewDreamer achieves the best generation quality among the models evaluated and improves cross-view consistency over independent per-view generation. These gains carry over to our world-action model (WAM) and action-conditioned world model (AC-WM). Our WAM outperforms the FastWAM-Joint baseline on LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0, and RoboDojo, and on a real Franka robot. Both variants improve out-of-domain generalization to various camera settings and scenes.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《SonoAtlasWorld: Anatomy-Grounded Ultrasound World Models for Wide-Range Scanning》归入 世界模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AdaWorld, CheXWorld, EchoWorld, LiveWorld, SonoAtlasWorld, SonoGym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning robotic ultrasound policies requires interactive environments for scalable data generation and repeatable evaluation, while exploration on real patients is limited by acquisition costs and safety constraints. Generative world models offer an alternative, but image history and probe actions alone do not ensure anatomical consistency across wide-range scans and changing acoustic windows. We introduce SonoAtlasWorld, an ultrasound world model built on anatomy as state. A persistent 3D anatomical asset extracted from computed tomography (CT) anchors each scan to a specific anatomy. Probe actions determine anatomical and acoustic visibility conditions for a causal ultrasound generator trained on clinical videos. Our three-stage pipeline combines anatomy-conditioned pretraining, causal conversion and long-rollout alignment. Inference optimizations achieve pose-to-ultrasound throughput of 81.2 FPS. To evaluate anatomical control beyond image similarity, we introduce the Action-Following Index (AFI), which measures normalized reconstruction gain over a static-prefix baseline. On 40 held-out 3D volumes, SonoAtlasWorld achieves liver and macro-organ AFI of 0.428 and 0.262, compared with at most 0.117 and 0.038 for the tested action- and geometry-conditioned methods. Long-rollout alignment reduces brightness drift by 3.46x at the end of 40 repeated scan cycles. SonoAtlasWorld also generates offline policy-training demonstrations and supports anatomically grounded closed-loop evaluation. Scaling the generated training data improves key-plane acquisition success from 48.2% to 81.2% on unseen anatomies in simulation, demonstrating its utility for robotic ultrasound policy learning.

</details>

![[assets/2611.26825_figure.png|800]]

- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26825

- **作者**: Tomás Couso Coddou, Christ Devia, Rex G. Liu, Rodrigo Toro Icarte
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Skill-JEPA: Learning Planner-Aligned Action Spaces for World Models》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OGBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models based on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn from pixels to predict the outcome of actions in a latent space. To reach a goal, a planner samples many candidate action sequences, uses the model to predict where each one leads, and executes the best one. However, standard planners draw their candidates from a Gaussian, while the model has only seen the structured, state-dependent actions of its training data. Its predictions for such candidates are unreliable, and planning fails more often as goals move farther away. We propose Skill-JEPA, which learns an action space aligned with the planner. A variational autoencoder conditioned on the current state encodes short action sequences into latent skills with a standard Gaussian prior, and decodes them back into actions. The world model predicts the effect of skills rather than raw actions, and the planner searches over skills starting from that same Gaussian, so its candidates resemble the model's training data. Skill-JEPA adds a single module, trained end-to-end with the world model, and leaves the planner untouched, with no subgoals, hierarchy, or hand-designed skills. Across three JEPA world models and four benchmarks, average success on goals 80 steps away rises from 42% with raw actions to 62%; the gains grow with goal distance and hold across four planners. On AntMaze, a quadruped locomotion task, success on goals two maze cells away rises from 0.4% to 38%. This suggests that part of the difficulty of reaching distant goals lies in the action space, and can be removed without changing the planner.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AudioSet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Neuromorphic sensing and computing offer a promising alternative to conventional deep learning pipelines for mobile robots, where perception must run continuously under tight latency and energy constraints. However, most neuromorphic solutions are still evaluated offline or on isolated benchmarks, and their integration into complete robotic systems remains largely unexplored. In this technical report, a four-wheel rover platform is presented that combines three real-time perception pipelines: a classical audio tagging pipeline based on Pretrained Audio Neural Networks (PANNs) running on an NVIDIA Jetson GPU, a neuromorphic audio pipeline in which a spiking neural network (SNN) is deployed on the SynSense Xylo Audio~2 chip, and a neuromorphic vision pipeline in which events from an event-based camera are processed by the SpikeYOLO spiking detector for traffic cone detection. On the Xylo chip, the whole processing chain---from the microphone signal to the classification of low- and high-pitched sounds---is performed on-chip, while the host only configures the device and reads the output neurons. All models are deployed as containerised services and integrated through ROS~2, and a configurable action dispatcher maps the detected classes to robot behaviours, closing the loop between perception and actuation. The platform is demonstrated in a proof-of-concept scenario in which the robot reacts to sound classes detected by both audio pipelines and detects traffic cones with the event camera, showing the feasibility of integrating neuromorphic hardware into a multimodal robotic platform operating in real time.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/What_Must_a_Model_Tell_a_Planner_Outcome_Interfaces_for_Risk-Aware_Planning_After_Robot_Damage|What Must a Model Tell a Planner? Outcome Interfaces for Risk-Aware Planning After Robot Damage]]

![[assets/2611.27939_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27939](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27939)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27939
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/What_Must_a_Model_Tell_a_Planner_Outcome_Interfaces_for_Risk-Aware_Planning_After_Robot_Damage|What Must a Model Tell a Planner? Outcome Interfaces for Risk-Aware Planning After Robot Damage]]
- **作者**: Hassan Iqbal, Krishna Kumar
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.6（加权：具身智能 0.3，世界模型 0.2，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《What Must a Model Tell a Planner? Outcome Interfaces for Risk-Aware Planning After Robot Damage》归入 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

When a robot is damaged, it must decide which routes it can still afford, and the amount of risk it may accept can change from one mission to the next. We argue that whether a learned model helps such a robot depends on its decision interface, that is, on what the model hands to the planner. A decision policy tells the planner what to do, whereas an outcome model, a kind of world model, predicts the consequences of each route. We study simulated Unitree Go2 quadrupeds and H1 humanoids with weakened motors, added payloads, or reduced foot friction. We compare four interfaces. The first is a decision policy, and the second is the same policy trained with fixed penalties for infeasible routes. The third is an outcome model that predicts the feasibility and travel time of each route. The fourth, which we call a capability contract, is an outcome model that predicts the chance of success and the travel time of the robot on each terrain and composes them along a route. All share the same collected experience, matched tuning and risk calibration, and an uncertainty guard. Two comparatively low-risk probes let the capability contract rank hazardous terrains about as well as four new trials on every hazard. At a calibrated 5% limit on selecting empirically infeasible routes, outcome models cut declined missions from about 32% to 7-8% on Go2 and from 57% to 35-36% on H1 compared with the plain policy. Fixed penalties narrow this gap, but the best penalty changes with the risk target. Predicting outcomes lets the planner preserve missions by changing routes as risk requirements tighten.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27817_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27817)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27817

- **作者**: Adolfo Damiano Cafaro, Patrizio Mariani, Roberto Galeazzi
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.4（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 1.1）

#### 研究背景与动机

《BRINA: Bottom-Referenced Inertial Navigation with AKAZE, a Sonar SLAM for Autonomous Marine Vehicles》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Forward-looking sonar (FLS) provides reliable underwater perception where optical sensing is limited, but its use for navigation is hindered by the unobserved elevation of each return. As a result, motion estimated directly from sonar images suffers from an unknown metric scale that is commonly resolved using a Doppler velocity log (DVL). We present BRINA (Bottom-Referenced Inertial Navigation with AKAZE), a sonar-inertial SLAM system for marine vehicles that uses the FLS as its only exteroceptive sensor. BRINA estimates the sonar altitude from the first seabed return in each beam and uses it to project matched features onto the seabed, providing metric odometry without a DVL. We further derive a bound relating the remaining scale error to seabed tilt and slope variability. The method is evaluated in simulation with an autonomous underwater vehicle, and in field experiments with uncrewed surface vehicles over ten missions, using two FLS systems under different operating conditions.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning factories have been increasingly applied in engineering research and education. In this paper, we develop a small-scale, low-cost, fully open-sourced learning factory that leverages AI and digital twins to demonstrate smart and resilient manufacturing. The learning factory consists of an AGV, three robots, two conveyors and their digital twins. Three typical use cases are designed to demonstrate the capabilities of the learning factory, i.e., material sorting through computer vision-based robot control, coordinating AGV, robots and conveyors for assembly, and digital twin-driven predictive maintenance. The results show that the developed learning factory can adequately support the teaching and research activities on digital twins and AI on manufacturing. Its fully open-source architecture and relatively low deployment cost make it an ideal solution for universities and research institutions.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27540_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27540](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27540)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27540
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Where_Delay_Compensation_Belongs_in_a_Decentralized_Mean-Field-Game_Swarm_with_a_Control-Barrier_Safety_Filter|Where Delay Compensation Belongs in a Decentralized Mean-Field-Game Swarm with a Control-Barrier Safety Filter]]
- **作者**: Jan Mikołajczyk, Igor Rostkowski, Bartosz Ogórkiewicz, Szymon Skierski, Sylwia Opat, Wojciech Rybak, Mikołaj Sczogiel, Radosław Pałys, Jan Gogulski, Bartosz Kościołek, Andrzej Pijanowski, Szymon Kowalski...
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.2（加权：具身智能 0.3，大模型 0.2，机器人 0.7）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《Where Delay Compensation Belongs in a Decentralized Mean-Field-Game Swarm with a Control-Barrier Safety Filter》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large robot teams are often run as a plan-then-execute stack. We study a decentralized instance for drone swarms, a mean-field-game (MFG) routing field with a per-agent control-barrier-function (CBF) safety filter, when communication is limited by a shared TDMA channel. Neighbors are known only through noisy range and bearing measurements exchanged in round-robin slots, so the frame, and with it the age of every estimate, grows with swarm size. In simulation this delay reaches both layers but breaks only the safety layer. Without delay, swarms of up to 100 agents do not collide, even with each measurement's noise held for a whole frame; with it, stale estimates at the CBF cause collisions beyond a frame period of 125 to 175 ms, or 75 to 100 ms at 100 agents with held noise, and swarm size sets where that limit lies and how steeply collisions rise. At the MFG, staleness makes the local density noisier without biasing it, and throughput drops by at most a tenth. We then compensate with lightweight on-board predictors, from constant-velocity fits to a small learned network, that estimate each neighbor's position from its last broadcasts. A layer-split experiment shows where prediction is needed: at the CBF it restores safety and extends the tolerated frame period by 1.4 to 1.7 times with an untrained constant-velocity fit and about 2.3 with the learned network when the noise is redrawn each step; with noise held for a frame the two extend it about equally, while at the MFG it only refines the congestion correction. A constant wider safety margin, the simplest alternative, makes collisions more frequent in a bottleneck. With the routing solved offline, that layer is robust to the delay because it sees neighbors only through a crowd average; compensation belongs in the safety filter, switched on only above the uncompensated limit, since below it the predictor's own error adds collisions.

</details>

《Ego4Gen: Generating Geometry-Grounded Motion Tokens as a World-Action Interface》归入 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present Ego4Gen (Egocentric 4D Future Generation), a world model for 4D future generation in robotic manipu lation. Given a single egocentric image and a text prompt, Ego4Gen jointly generates temporally aligned RGB frames, dense point maps, and dense scene flow, explicitly pre dicting the evolution of scene appearance, spatial struc ture, and motion. Built on Cosmos3-Nano, Ego4Gen ex tends the pretrained generative backbone through modality routed LoRA and asymmetric attention, using geometric pseudo-labels from MotionCrafter to learn to predict future geometry and motion latents. These latents can either be decoded into explicit 3D structure and motion or mapped to robot actions. To this end, we also introduce FlowIn verse, a lightweight inverse dynamics network trained in two stages to translate predicted 4D latents into robot ac tions. Experiments show that these geometry and motion latents outperform the evaluated latent-action and video latent baselines in out-of-distribution settings. Closed loop policy evaluations further demonstrate their utility for translating predicted 4D futures into robot control. To gether, these results support geometry-grounded motion to kens as a world-action interface, connecting 4D future gen eration with robotic manipulation and suggesting a path to ward reduced reliance on action-labeled data.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Frontier large language models (LLMs) have enabled vision-and-language navigation (VLN) through direct closed-loop perception and action. However, navigation performance depends strongly on the harness governing LLM-environment interactions, while existing harnesses largely rely on manually designed navigation tools, memory, and agent roles. We introduce NavRSI, a harness-centric recursive self-improvement framework that optimizes the navigation behavior of frozen LLMs through closed-loop experience. In each round, NavRSI iteratively collects navigation trajectories and failure diagnostics, employs a designer agent to rewrite the harness, and retains the revisions that prove effective, while the designer accumulates experience from previous revisions so that the harness and this experience co-evolve. Privileged simulator diagnostics guide only the designer and remain inaccessible to the navigating LLM, enabling deployment with a single RGB camera and primitive actions, without task-specific model training or additional navigation tools. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements across LLMs from four vendors, with 85.64% success rate on the full R2R-CE val-unseen split with GPT-6-Astra, surpassing the previously reported best by 4.13%. Evaluations on RxR-CE, NavRAG-CE, and a real humanoid robot further demonstrate its generalizability.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Path-Aware_Fusion_of_Infrastructure_RF_Sensing_and_Robot_Perception_in_a_Live_Robotic_Digital_Twin|Path-Aware Fusion of Infrastructure RF Sensing and Robot Perception in a Live Robotic Digital Twin]]

![[assets/2611.27497_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.NI
