#### 研究背景与动机

《Path-Aware Fusion of Infrastructure RF Sensing and Robot Perception in a Live Robotic Digital Twin》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Networking and Internet Architecture 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Mobile robots in industrial workspaces must react to people that their onboard sensors cannot yet observe. Communication infrastructure can fill part of this gap by acting as a sensor, but its observations differ from camera and LiDAR evidence in meaning, spatial support, and rate. We present a live robotic Digital Twin (DT) that integrates heterogeneous sensing branches, a commodity 60-GHz link, joint Wi-Fi/5G sensing, a fixed camera, and robot-borne LiDAR, as spatially grounded observations with explicit freshness, and that uses the robot's live pose and heading to decide whether evidence lies on the path the robot is about to sweep. We show that a 60-GHz link carrying near-gigabit traffic detects human presence through the phase signature of its antenna array (balanced accuracy 0.98 on unseen sessions and 0.999 from one day to the next in a first site), but that a second site with a different link geometry requires a site-specific model (0.98 on unseen sessions after retraining, 0.73 without). On a Unitree Go2 patrolling under the DT, every person entering the camera field or the 60-GHz link stopped the robot (20/20); for the camera, the median time from physical entry to standstill was 1.83 s, of which detection, DT update, and networking take about 0.15 s and the rest is the 0.5-s confirmation and the robot's braking. Path-aware gating removed all stops caused by off-path presence (5 stops versus 0 for the same scenario), stale branches stopped the robot in fail-safe mode (6/6) and were ignored otherwise (0/6), and five unattended laps produced no false stop.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Efficient_Natural_Language_to_Temporal_Logic_Translation_for_Real-Time_Physical_AI|Efficient Natural Language to Temporal Logic Translation for Real-Time Physical AI]]

![[assets/2611.27489_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27489](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27489)

#### 研究背景与动机

《Efficient Natural Language to Temporal Logic Translation for Real-Time Physical AI》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots can be controlled to perform tasks with guaranteed correctness when the task instructions are stated in a formal language. Large language models (LLMs) have been widely used to allow users to provide such instructions in natural language (NL), by translating NL into formal language. When deploying such translators on a robot, however, we face limited computing and energy resources, while offloading computation or data to the cloud may be infeasible or undesirable. Yet an LLM is a general-purpose text generator, whereas a formula in a fixed formal language fragment follows a well-defined syntax. We therefore ask whether text generation is needed at all. For the formal language of linear temporal logic (LTL), we show that this translation can instead be broken down into answering a set of questions with pre-defined answers. We then construct a System-One model, a recent class of NL-based classifier, to provide such answers and enable us to translate a natural language instruction into an LTL instruction. On three benchmarks, our translator matches or exceeds the accuracy of the best local LLMs available, at up to one order of magnitude lower runtime and energy consumption. We show that it generalizes to unseen atomic propositions and temporal patterns.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/Ego2Ego_Generative_Visual_Perspective_Taking_Across_Collaborators|Ego2Ego: Generative Visual Perspective Taking Across Collaborators]]

![[assets/2611.27459_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27459](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27459)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27459
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/Ego2Ego_Generative_Visual_Perspective_Taking_Across_Collaborators|Ego2Ego: Generative Visual Perspective Taking Across Collaborators]]
- **作者**: Chuhan Jiao, John Gideon, Anshul Gupta, Guy Rosman, Andreas Bulling
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 0.8（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 0.5）

#### 研究背景与动机

《Ego2Ego: Generative Visual Perspective Taking Across Collaborators》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ME-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visual perspective taking, the ability to infer what another person sees from their position, is fundamental to human collaboration. However, prior works reduced perspective taking to state estimation, geometric reconstruction, or discrete question answering, whereas existing cross-view video generation methods either inferred the target view from the input video alone or required an external camera that observes the target person. We introduce generative visual perspective taking: Given an ego person's egocentric video, a scene point cloud, and a collaborator's camera trajectory, the task is to generate the continuous egocentric video from the collaborator's perspective. This task is challenging given extreme viewpoint shifts, dynamic occlusions, and sparse visual overlap. We address these challenges with Ego2Ego -- a set of backbone-agnostic geometric modules that provide a pretrained video diffusion model with the 3D scene geometry shared by both interactants. Ego2Ego renders dense, temporally consistent scene coordinate maps into every view as a shared spatial reference, aligns cross-view tokens in 3D ray space through relative geometric attention, and recovers regions outside the ego person's current view from the ego person's visual memory. Experiments on the CoMind dataset across three video diffusion backbones show that Ego2Ego consistently improves visual quality, Level-2 scene layout accuracy, and collaborator motion, outperforming state-of-the-art cross-view video generation baselines on every backbone. By generating the collaborator's egocentric video more accurately, Ego2Ego brings visual perspective taking to human-robot collaboration, where it can help infer what a human collaborator sees and does, and can turn single-person recordings into synchronised multi-perspective videos for robot learning.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/GenPA_Image_Generators_as_Efficient_Robot_Policies|GenPA: Image Generators as Efficient Robot Policies]]

![[assets/2611.27446_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《GenPA: Image Generators as Efficient Robot Policies》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Pretrained visual generation models have shown great promise in robotic manipulation. However, a representative line of work still treats future visual prediction as the central link between visual generative pretraining and action learning, retaining it as a training objective even when future scenes are no longer generated at inference time. We argue that the instruction-following, spatial understanding, and visual imagination capabilities acquired through image generation pretraining can support control without future visual prediction: image generation models themselves can be directly adapted into powerful action learners. Building on this perspective, we propose GenPA, which fine-tunes an image generation backbone with a lightweight 19M-parameter action regression adapter and reuses its native generative objective for perception co-training. The native image-editing interface accommodates different visual targets without task-specific perception heads, enabling comparisons of current-scene perception, future prediction, and their combinations within a common policy architecture. At deployment, GenPA requires only a single backbone forward pass and action regression, without visual generation or iterative action denoising. Without additional large-scale robot-data pretraining, GenPA demonstrates strong control performance and robustness to perturbations across simulation benchmarks (99.10% success on LIBERO and 83.79% on LIBERO-Plus) and real-world manipulation tasks. Under compiled inference, GenPA takes 20.9 ms per action chunk on a single H100 GPU, achieving 1.7-3.4x speedups over strong VLA and WAM baselines. Our latency analysis further highlights architectural advantages of visual generation backbones for efficient robot control. Ablations across visual tasks and their combinations identify current-depth supervision as a simple, effective training recipe.

</details>

《ADEPT: Adaptive Monocular Depth Estimation via Model-In-The-Loop Photometric Tuning with Capture Risk Prediction》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Exposure and gain determine the visual evidence available to monocular depth estimation (MDE), particularly when robot motion creates a tradeoff between light collection and motion blur. We present ADEPT, a framework that adapts these settings using learned feedback from a frozen depth model. During training, ground-truth depth identifies the lowest-error reference within a group of physically evaluated camera settings. A compact predictor learns reference-relative disagreement (RRD) between each candidate's depth prediction and the reference prediction using regression and ranking losses. At deployment, it scores observed captures using frozen-model features and camera, motion, and illumination context, without ground truth or reference images. A contextual Gaussian-process controller uses recent scores to select subsequent exposure and gain settings under motion and photometric constraints. We evaluate offline camera selection with relative and metric Depth Anything v2 and demonstrate online control with the affine-invariant variant on a mobile robot. Across nine equally weighted scene-illumination conditions, ADEPT reduces mean scale-and-shift-aligned $\mathrm{Abs}_{\mathrm{rel}}$ by 14.6% relative to built-in auto-exposure and by 10.0% relative to the best-performing evaluated prior controller.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The structural integrity of wind turbine blades is critical for safe, efficient energy production. Accurate 3D reconstruction allows precise localization and quantification of wear and early damage detection. While drone-based inspections are standard, their high-resolution imagery poses challenges such as weak texture, lighting variability, and high computational cost. We present BladeR, a scalable pipeline for high-quality 3D reconstruction from inspection imagery. A hierarchical sparse reconstruction strategy leverages GPS/IMU data and blade shape priors to guide advanced keypoint matchers on full-resolution images. For dense reconstruction, a tiled Gaussian Splatting framework processes 45 MP images at native resolution, isolates the blade from background, and adaptively enhances detail recovery along the leading edge. Validation against commercial and open-source baselines shows significant improvements in model completeness and detail.

</details>

- **作者**: Bingyu Li, Lichang Yang, Zhiyuan Zhao, Junyu Gao, Xuelong Li
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 0.8（加权：机器人 0.8）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《PanoUAV-Track: Full-Spherical UAV Visual Tracking in the Wild》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：TrackingNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Existing UAV tracking benchmarks predominantly rely on perspective cameras with limited fields of view. Omnidirectional cameras enable full-spherical observation, yet tracking from moving UAV platforms remains largely unexplored. Unlike ground-level panoramic tracking, UAV-mounted omnidirectional cameras observe targets across both azimuth and elevation, introducing geometric challenges under equirectangular projection (ERP). To investigate this setting, we capture approximately \textbf{40 hours of real-world 8K panoramic UAV video} and construct \textbf{PanoUAV-Track} through temporal clip extraction, target annotation, and the integration of simulated sequences. The resulting benchmark comprises 1,895 sequences, 507,961 frames, and 2,872 annotated objects, covering real-world daytime and nighttime scenes alongside simulation, and supporting both visual and language-based target specification. Through extensive evaluations, we reveal a clear \textbf{perspective-to-spherical} generalization gap and identify tracking degradation associated with latitude-dependent distortion and ERP seam discontinuity. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{PanoUAV-Tracker (PUTrack)}, a geometry-aware tracking framework combining latitude-adaptive search and seam-aware periodic sampling. PUTrack improves zero-shot and in-domain tracking, particularly for seam-crossing targets. Code and benchmark resources will be available\footnote{\textcolor{tabpink}{https://libingyu01.github.io/PanoUAV-Track/}}.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/GeoSDF-Diff_Geometry-Aware_Diffusion_Planning_for_Collision-Free_Trajectories|GeoSDF-Diff: Geometry-Aware Diffusion Planning for Collision-Free Trajectories]]

![[assets/2611.27373_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27373)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27373
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/GeoSDF-Diff_Geometry-Aware_Diffusion_Planning_for_Collision-Free_Trajectories|GeoSDF-Diff: Geometry-Aware Diffusion Planning for Collision-Free Trajectories]]
- **作者**: Yueming Li, Zhanjiang Yang, Yang Shen, Meng Li, Lijun Sun
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 0.8（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 0.5）

#### 研究背景与动机

《GeoSDF-Diff: Geometry-Aware Diffusion Planning for Collision-Free Trajectories》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Diffusion models have recently attracted increasing attention in motion planning due to their ability to model high-dimensional and multi-modal trajectory distributions. However, existing diffusion-based planners still struggle to reliably satisfy geometric feasibility in unseen cluttered environments with complex obstacle layouts. To address this challenge, we propose GeoSDF-Diff, a geometry-aware diffusion planner that incorporates multi-scale SDF conditioning into trajectory denoising. The key idea is to provide the denoiser with both route-level geometric context and fine local clearance evidence, enabling generated trajectories to better adapt to unseen complex obstacle layouts. Specifically, a Geometric Encoder extracts multi-scale SDF features and injects them into a DiT-X denoiser through cross-attention. We further introduce a trajectory-level training objective that combines SDF clearance, violation emphasis, smoothness, and path-length regularization, so that local infeasibilities are penalized during training rather than corrected only at inference time. Experiments on held-out cluttered environments show that, without post-hoc trajectory repair or sampling-time guidance, GeoSDF-Diff achieves a 65.2% average success rate across diverse test scenarios, outperforming strong diffusion baselines by 25.4% to 28.5%.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/NormFlowSDE_Normalizing_Flows_for_Amortized_Bayesian_State_Estimation|NormFlowSDE: Normalizing Flows for Amortized Bayesian State Estimation]]

![[assets/2611.27362_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.27362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27362)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.27362
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/NormFlowSDE_Normalizing_Flows_for_Amortized_Bayesian_State_Estimation|NormFlowSDE: Normalizing Flows for Amortized Bayesian State Estimation]]
- **作者**: Yi Zhang, Lars Mikelsons
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 0.8（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《NormFlowSDE: Normalizing Flows for Amortized Bayesian State Estimation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Bayesian state estimators, such as Kalman filters, estimate states from noisy observations based on dynamical models. However, obtaining accurate models can be challenging when the system involves effects that are difficult to model faithfully, such as friction, impacts, and deformation in contact-rich manipulation. Uncertainty in system parameters, inputs, and disturbances, together with simplified noise models, can further introduce simulation-to-reality mismatch and degrade estimation accuracy. These limitations motivate learning a distribution over state trajectories conditioned on observations and controls without explicitly specifying the underlying physical models. We introduce NormFlowSDE, an amortized Bayesian state estimator that learns a distribution over state trajectories by combining neural stochastic differential equations with conditional normalizing flows. Its key design choice is to utilize the capability of normalizing flows to learn stochastic behavior of variables, while leveraging the continuous-time dynamics of SDEs to model the evolution of states over time. Once trained, the model draws state paths for new measurement sequences without per-sequence optimization. In addition, we connect NormFlowSDE to stochastic continuous-time dynamics by proving weak convergence of a mean-centered, drift-corrected inference scheme to an Itô diffusion under stated assumptions. Finally, we benchmark NormFlowSDE against direct estimators and learned particle filters based on normalizing flows on partially observed dynamical systems, including simulated systems and real-world robot datasets. The results show that NormFlowSDE attains the lowest estimation error with calibrated uncertainty intervals on both robot datasets and filters four times faster than the fastest particle filter on every dataset.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Dual-Agent DRL based Cooperative Beamforming and Trajectory Optimization for UAV-ISAC Networks》归入 机器人、强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Information Theory 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL, MADRL, MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper considers a low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) network, where multiple ground base stations (BSs) provide integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) services for multiple communication UAVs (C-UAVs) and sensing UAVs (S-UAVs). With the objective of maximizing the average sum rate of C-UAVs over the entire flight episode, we jointly optimize the multi-BS multi-UAV ISAC beamforming and trajectory design, subject to the BS transmit power budget and the sensing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) constraints of S-UAVs, as well as the flight mission and collision avoidance constraints of C-UAVs and S-UAVs. Based on the multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MADRL) technique, we develop a dual-agent scheme, termed DeepDA-OPT, to solve the resulting constrained Markov decision process (MDP). In the proposed DeepDA-OPT scheme, two agents, i.e., Agent-B and Agent-T, are specifically deployed for learning the multi-BS ISAC transmit beamformers and the C-UAV/S-UAV trajectories, respectively, where the optimal BS receive beamformers for the sensing echo signals of S-UAVs are obtained in closed form by leveraging the generalized Rayleigh quotient theorem. To mitigate the overestimation of value functions, both Agent-B and Agent-T are trained based on the twin-delayed deep deterministic (TD3) policy-gradient algorithm. In addition, a constraint-aware noise exploration policy is employed, and the reward function is designed to incorporate a sensing penalty and a flight urgency penalty, thereby guiding the training of Agent-B and Agent-T. Numerical results show that, compared with the baseline schemes, the proposed DeepDA-OPT scheme achieves a higher average sum rate of C-UAVs, while the sensing SINR constraints are empirically well satisfied and no collision violation is observed under the evaluated settings.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning Interaction Dynamics via Motion-Region Guidance for Robotic Video World Models》归入 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Ctrl-World, GigaWorld, MoReWorld, PointWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Action-conditioned video world models imagine future observations under candidate robot actions, providing a promising visual tool for robot policy evaluation and interaction simulation. However, existing video world models often optimize global reconstruction errors instead of emphasizing interaction-relevant regions. As a result, they may preserve overall scene appearance but overlook local interaction details, leading to physically implausible outcomes such as hallucinated contact effects and inconsistent robot-object interactions. We introduce MoReWorld, a unified motion-region-guided framework that uses a shared motion region, the part of the environment that moves in response to the robot's actions, to focus dynamics learning and video generation on interaction-relevant regions. The same motion region guides local motion prediction, region-aware supervision, and motion-gated future-feature conditioning. At inference time, the motion region is predicted as a motion probability map from visual history and robot actions, without requiring future observations or ground-truth motion labels. On WorldSimProbe, MoReWorld achieves the highest overall simulator-faithfulness score, with the strongest performance on local consistency, global consistency, and interaction dynamics. These results indicate that motion-region guidance helps the model preserve scene-level consistency while more faithfully predicting action-conditioned interaction dynamics.

</details>

<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper introduces Frontier Voltage Boost Laplacian path planning. The method inherits many of the attractive features of earlier Harmonic Potential Field planning methods, including absence of local minima and smooth paths, while improving the original. The piecewise approach we introduce is able to solve larger maps and handle narrow passages more quickly than earlier Harmonic Potential Field methods. After explaining the algorithm, we experimentally characterize its cor- rectness, performance, and path smoothness as a function of map size. Code is available at https://github.com/vpritamani/FrontierVoltageBoostLaplacianPathPlanning

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Completing a manipulation task does not establish that a robot follows the requested goal rather than a familiar routine. ManipScope is an evaluation-only diagnostic benchmark for robotic instruction following under changed task requirements. Built on RoboTwin 2.0's simulation resources, it specifies 48 held-out scenes across six task families with explicit source-task links; policies are trained on unmodified RoboTwin 2.0 demonstrations, and no demonstrations of the scenes are provided, so success requires reusing skills under a changed request rather than fitting supplied examples. Beyond task success, ManipScope provides a chain of diagnostics in which each step tests one explanation of a failure. For twelve policy checkpoints, 84.6% of failures in 13,200 episodes on eleven selection scenes displace a distractor, and gripper records show that failed episodes usually grasp a non-target. When the target is reassigned among the candidates on identical initial states, ten checkpoints follow every instruction on 3.9% of 1,000 states, below the 16% of an instruction-blind chooser, while a GPT-6 agent run as positive control reaches 88%. The failure recurs on unmodified training tasks and is not explained by missing manipulation skills, so success in these scenes largely reflects instruction-independent preferences; two checkpoints trained on the same data nevertheless follow color instructions. In a feasibility test, letting an agent select the target and the policy execute partly restores instruction following. Case studies with future-video and internal-intervention probes further show that forecasts and execution can agree on the wrong target and that action changes need not transfer the goal. Success, consistency, and sensitivity must each be checked against the requested goal.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/HermiteTwin_Bicubic_Hermite_Shell_Digital_Twins_for_Contact-Rich_Deformable_Manipulation|HermiteTwin: Bicubic Hermite Shell Digital Twins for Contact-Rich Deformable Manipulation]]

![[assets/2611.27282_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《HermiteTwin: Bicubic Hermite Shell Digital Twins for Contact-Rich Deformable Manipulation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GauSim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

While existing methods for reconstructing deformable object manipulation have made impressive progress, they typically represent a thin object with a single particle set that must serve two purposes at once: modeling bending, and resolving contact with the robot. To bridge this gap, we present HermiteTwin, a framework that reconstructs a physical digital twin of a thin deformable object from multiview video of a robot manipulating it. Our key idea is to represent deformation and contact separately, each at its own resolution. The object is simulated as a bicubic Hermite shell, a coarse grid of $C^1$ elements whose nodal positions and derivatives define a smooth surface with Kirchhoff--Love bending, while the gripper acts through dense samples in material space that are captured, clamped, and released, and whose attachment forces reach the shell through the Hermite basis. This decoupling keeps the physical state compact while contact stays fine-grained. We identify effective material and contact parameters with CMA-ES followed by adjoint-based refinement, and drive a reconstructed Gaussian appearance model with the simulated shell. In experiments on real cloth and foam manipulation, HermiteTwin outperforms the state-of-the-art spring--mass twin across reconstruction, resimulation, and future prediction.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper presents an efficient and robust approach for autonomous aerial exploration in complex environments. Instead of relying on conventional 3D voxel-based maps for exploration, we utilize a lightweight 2.5D map framework that reduces computational overhead while preserving essential topographic information. More importantly, this representation systematically eliminates the blind voxel problem commonly encountered in voxel-based exploration due to occlusions and limited visibility. Unlike previous methods that rely on a fixed altitude scale for exploration, our approach requires no prior height information and actively adapts to terrain elevation changes during exploration. To further enhance exploration efficiency while preserving the generality of 3D exploration, we perform 2D path planning on the 2.5D map framework, which is then lifted to 3D to evaluate the cost between viewpoints by taking into account terrain traversability, thereby reducing frequent altitude changes and enabling smoother exploration. Extensive experiments conducted in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and advantages of our approach over existing solutions. Our approach demonstrates fast exploration of over 2000~$\text{m}^2$ of cluttered forest and sloped mountainous terrain with elevation changes of up to 10~m, reaching a maximum speed of 5~m/s in real-world tests.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《NavTTT: Adaptive Parametric Memory via Hierarchical Test-Time Training for Vision-and-Language Navigation》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) demands that agents accumulate and reason over streaming visual observations throughout long navigation episodes. Existing approaches struggle to maintain long-term history, constrained by the finite context capacity of transformer architectures. To solve the problem, fast-weight test-time training (TTT) provides a mechanism for encoding streaming observations into an evolving parameter state. However, existing TTT does not distinguish informative observations from repetitive visual content, allowing redundant tokens to interfere with the retention of information needed for subsequent decisions. We propose Navigation-Conditioned Test-Time Training (NavTTT), which introduces adaptive memory formation and hierarchical memory integration for vision-and-language navigation. NavTTT leverages global navigation context to prioritize informative visual evidence and integrates language instructions with spatial information to construct spatially grounded memory representations. Episode-local nonlinear fast weights store these representations as Adaptive Parametric Memory (APM), preserving history beyond the visual context for later decisions. To reduce cross-layer coupling in memory formation, NavTTT maintains independent fast-weight memories across multiple decoder depths. This decoupled design prevents errors or biases from spreading across different memory levels. Meanwhile, it enables shared historical information to be independently represented at various levels of abstraction. Experiments demonstrate strong navigation performance on VLN-CE benchmarks and effective instruction following on a real robot.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27182_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Sunli Chen, Yun Zong, Qinhong Zhou, Xiaowen Qiu, Jiaben Chen, Zeyuan Yang, Chuang Gan

#### 研究背景与动机

《Tripwire: Robot Agents that Program Their Own Interrupts under Human Disturbance》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Agent harnesses are turn-based: the agent acts, waits for the result, then reasons. When the action invokes a pretrained robot policy, such as a vision-language-action (VLA) or world action model (WAM), the policy may react continuously to observations, but the agent cannot revisit its decision until the call returns. Meanwhile, a person may step into the robot's path or move the object it is about to grasp. Invoking the agent more often narrows this blind interval, but a reasoning model takes seconds per decision and most control steps need no new one. We propose TRIP, a training-free, event-driven harness in which the agent programs its own interrupts. With each call, the agent writes a tripwire: a lightweight, stateful Python program that runs alongside the policy, can query a compact VLM (for example, to locate a person and measure their distance), and decides whether the call continues or control returns to the agent. When it fires, the harness halts the policy and returns control with the reason; the agent then reassesses, adapts, and continues under a rewritten tripwire. We evaluate TRIP with three VLAs and one WAM on RoboHD, a benchmark of 168 RoboCasa365 scenes in which a simulated person disturbs the robot and any human-robot contact counts as failure. Compared with a turn-based harness using the same agent and tools, TRIP halves the rate of human contact with the strongest policy, raising safety from 61.3% to 81.3%, without sacrificing task success (38.7% vs. 31.0%). Real-robot experiments show that TRIP completes two manipulation tasks under human interference where the turn-based harness and the policy alone largely fail.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Inject, Encode, Navigate: Feature-Wise Time Injection for Rectified Flow with Bidirectional Feature Encoding》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World, UNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-model-based robotic navigation has gained increasing popularity, yet existing approaches still struggle to deliver effective and efficient navigation performance, \textit{e.g.}, diffusion-based policies that require costly iterative denoising. In this work, we propose three improvements to bridge these gaps. First, we adopt a rectified flow framework to reduce the required inference steps to one while achieving comparable or even superior performance. Second, we leverage a bidirectional Mamba-based visual encoder that employs selective state-space modeling to more effectively capture bidirectional temporal dependencies across observation frames, even with longer input sequences. Most importantly, we find that naïvely combining these two components to balance accuracy with inference speed yields no further performance gains or even produces inferior results. Upon extensive analysis, we realized that the root cause lies in the fact that the Hydra-derived temporal context is shared unchanged across all flow timesteps, and timestep information is introduced only within the velocity network. This weak coupling limits the model's ability to adaptively exploit temporal visual features at different flow states. To address this, we propose a feature-wise timestep conditioning design that explicitly conditions the visual embedding on the flow timestep, enabling noise-level-aware trajectory generation, thereby restoring a meaningful gradient pathway and enabling coherent interaction between the rectified flow and the temporal encoder. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms both baseline and competitive methods.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Simulation-Based_Testing_of_RCM-Constrained_Robotic_Software_via_Task_Manifolds|Simulation-Based Testing of RCM-Constrained Robotic Software via Task Manifolds]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Simulation-Based Testing of RCM-Constrained Robotic Software via Task Manifolds》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

System-level testing of surgical robots requires generating executable test scenarios that exercise diverse, safety-critical behaviors. However, remote-center-of-motion (RCM) surgical manipulators operate within tightly constrained workspaces governed by coupled task geometry, inverse kinematics, and motor limits. Direct sampling in motor-command or Cartesian spaces frequently wastes testing budgets on physically unfeasible configurations. To address this limitation, we present a manifold-centered testing framework that decouples feasible test-domain construction from downstream test-generation strategies. Candidate inputs are generated directly within this constructed feasible domain and mapped to lower-level joint commands for simulation execution. Offline trajectory safety oracles and a spatial reference grid then evaluate failure yield and coverage. We compare our task-manifold representation against Cartesian XYZ and principal component analysis (PCA) under random testing, systematic traversal, and genetic search strategies. Results demonstrate that the feasible task domain improves test-generation efficiency and enables systematic reference-cell coverage. Crucially, random and genetic search strategies detect a broader spectrum of failure patterns than baseline representations, revealing that complete reference-cell coverage alone does not exhaust intra-cell behavioral dynamics. Furthermore, differential testing between defective and patched controller versions reveals that targeted evolutionary search detects aggregate failure-rate shifts from software repairs. Finally, a cross-platform study on a 7-DoF Kinova Gen3 arm in MoveIt 2 confirms workflow transferability to kinematically redundant manipulators. Together, these findings highlight the necessity of combining feasibility-aware domain modeling with complementary search strategies for systematic testing of robot software.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Opto-Electronic_Hybrid_Reinforcement_Learning_Based_on_MZI_for_Robotic_Manipulator_Control|Opto-Electronic Hybrid Reinforcement Learning Based on MZI for Robotic Manipulator Control]]

- **作者**: Mengting Yu, Shuiying Xiang, Changjian Xie, Yonghang Chen, Haowen Zhao, Xingxing Guo, Zhijian Hao, Yahui Zhang, Yanan Han, Yue Hao
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 强化学习, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 3.0（加权：具身智能 0.9，强化学习 1，机器人 1.1）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Opto-Electronic Hybrid Reinforcement Learning Based on MZI for Robotic Manipulator Control》归入 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、强化学习、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Precise motion control for autonomous robots is a core technical enabler for the large-scale deployment of embodied intelligence. In particular, basic manipulation tasks, such as point-to-point reaching and target grasping with robotic manipulators, impose stringent requirements on control accuracy, real-time response, and energy efficiency for edge deployment. As a prevailing end-to-end control paradigm, reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated remarkable capabilities in autonomous learning and generalization for decision-making in continuous action spaces. Nevertheless, constrained by electronic computing architectures, it generally suffers from substantial computing overhead, high inference latency and limited energy efficiency, making low-power edge deployment challenging. Optical computing, benefiting from its high-speed parallelism, offers a promising route to break energy-efficiency bottlenecks; however, research on the integration of optical computing and RL remains at an early stage. In this work, we propose a Mach-Zehnder Interferometer (MZI)-based opto-electronic hybrid RL architecture. We adopted two algorithm structures: Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient combined with Hindsight Experience Replay (DDPG-HER) and Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient combined with HER (TD3-HER). We implemented the key layer linear computation of the spiking neural network (SNN) Actor on an optical MZI chip for the reaching task of a Franka Emika Panda robotic manipulator. The architecture is evaluated in the PyBullet simulation environment. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed architecture achieves an energy efficiency of 1.39 TOPS/W and an inference latency of 120ps while maintaining control accuracy, which provide a feasible pathway toward energy-efficient and precise control for robots.

</details>

![[assets/2611.27101_figure.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《EgoSurgHands: An Egocentric 3D Hand Pose Dataset & Benchmark for Open Surgery Training》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Surgical skill assessment, AR guidance, and robot learning from demonstration all benefit from 3D tracking of the surgeon's hands, yet no surgical dataset offers real egocentric articulated 3D hand pose. We introduce EgoSurgHands, a benchmark for real-captured egocentric hand pose estimation in open surgery training on synthetic pads. Captured with multi-sensor glasses, it contains 41,909 training hand-instance rows across 55 procedure recordings and 9,476 human-validated evaluation rows across 13 held-out recordings, spanning 13 tasks, 3 glove colors, and 4 trainees from first-year medical student to third-year resident. Each frame carries a multi-sensor stack: intrinsics, hand landmarks with per-hand confidence, timestamps, and eye gaze, plus SLAM extrinsics where SLAM succeeded. Evaluation labels combine sensor-fused pseudo-ground truth, human cross-review of every frame, surgeon adjudication of flagged cases, and an independent multi-view triangulated reference. Alongside the within-corpus, procedure-disjoint split, a leave-one-wearer-out protocol tested unseen trainees. Across eight methods evaluated zero-shot, from MANO-based reconstructors to the two-hand IntagHand and the keypoint-only MediaPipe Hands, egocentric pretraining was associated with stronger transfer on every held-out wearer. WildHands and JointTransformer had 18\% and 40\% lower PA-MPJPE, respectively, than the non-egocentric HaMeR. JointTransformer was the best performing model with an error of 14.95 mm. A substantial surgical domain gap remains, and the widely deployed MediaPipe Hands detected a hand in only 24.1\% of evaluation rows. As a baseline, a 58K-parameter residual corrector (JRN) on frozen backbones reached 10.96-14.85 mm PA-MPJPE on held-out wearers for the two ego-pretrained backbones. We release the dataset and benchmark code at https://egosurghands.github.io

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《PonderPounce-WM: When Does Predicting the Future Help Context-Dependent Robot Control?》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

How does future prediction affect context-dependent robot control? We introduce PonderPounce-WM, extending PonderPounce with three future tokens supervised to predict visual-feature changes. Its pretrained MLLM, Ponder, integrates history and demonstrations into continuous cognition for the action model, Pounce. We investigate token ordering and delivery to distinguish predictive supervision from direct future conditioning. On RoboMME, cognition-first ordering with cognition-only delivery improves success from 60.83% to 67.42%. With the same ordering, direct future-token delivery yields 64.00% success. Probes show increased future information in cognition while the tested past-event attributes remain largely decodable. Across four real-robot tasks, PonderPounce-WM achieves 67.44% mean success under asynchronous execution, compared with 60.98% for PonderPounce. These results suggest that future prediction can improve context-dependent control through auxiliary supervision without delivering future tokens to the action model.

</details>

- **作者**: Zhiyuan Li, Charles Eret, Dingsheng Liu, Xuan Zhao, Joshua Choi, Yangzheng Wu, Tongtong Cao, Yingxue Zhang, Amir Rasouli
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboPRO: A Perturbation-Resilient and Obstacle-Aware Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GenieSim, X-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Object manipulation in the real world requires robotic policies to be robust to contextual perturbations and capable of safe planning in cluttered scenes. However, the majority of existing robotics benchmarks contain clean tabletop environments with success rate as the sole metric, leaving two critical deployment questions unanswered: does the policy manipulate safely under real conditions and what does lead to its failure? To this end, we present RoboPRO, a Robotics benchmark for Perturbation-Resilient and Obstacle-aware planning, which unifies various axes of robustness in manipulation. RoboPRO comprises 80 diverse bimanual atomic and compositional tasks across four furnished indoor environments, with a total of 16,000 demonstrations and 5.6K diverse language instructions. The scenes are populated with different numbers of distractors (non-targets), ranging from clean, involving only target objects, to densely populated to impose both visual and operational clutter. Moreover, we propose two safety-aware event metrics to determine unsafe contact between the robot and the environment or distractors. Our evaluation protocol consists of a per-factor analysis of three key perturbations, namely visual, linguistic, and object-level, with different levels of distractor densities. We conduct extensive experiments on RoboPRO using state-of-the-art manipulation policies to analyze the impact of visual and operational clutter, perturbations, and instructional diversity. Our findings indicate clutter can drastically reduce success and increase non-target collision, different perturbations have different effects on the performance, and that training data composition affects overall performance. Our findings highlight the key challenges for manipulation in real-world conditions and motivate future research in this domain. Project page: https://robopro-bench.github.io/RoboPRO/

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《RoboCap-10k: A Metrically Accurate Egocentric Dataset for Robot Learning》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HumanNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Egocentric video is the most scalable source of manipulation data for robot learning, but its difficulty lies in producing high-quality supervision. Existing large corpora lack metric 3D labels or rely on heterogeneous devices, motion-capture labs, or a headset's built-in real-time tracker, whose accuracy is not reported for the released data. We introduce RoboCap-10k: 10,000 curated hours of unscripted manipulation recorded during workers' paid shifts in 11 countries, spanning 18 task families, 200 task types, and 2,000 tasks. Every hour is captured on one device, the RoboCap, a 250 g hat with six synchronized global-shutter cameras and dual IMUs. Online calibration brings every device below 1 px of epipolar error, visual-inertial SLAM yields gravity-aligned 6-DoF head trajectories, and a multi-view hand tracker yields metric cm-precise hand poses. Each episode is annotated with task and sub-task language captions. Project page: https://robocap10k.com

</details>

![[assets/2611.26865_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26865)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26865
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/FlowScope_3D_Scene_Flow_Pretraining_Improves_Surgical_Robot_Policies|FlowScope: 3D Scene Flow Pretraining Improves Surgical Robot Policies]]
- **作者**: John J. Han, Adam Schmidt, Jingpei Lu, Zooey He, Muhammad Abdullah Jamal, Jie Ying Wu, Omid Mohareri
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能
- **相关性评分**: 1.6（加权：具身智能 0.3，机器人 1.3）

#### 研究背景与动机

《FlowScope: 3D Scene Flow Pretraining Improves Surgical Robot Policies》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Imitation learning for surgical robots is limited by the cost of collecting demonstrations. Meanwhile, many robot-assisted procedures are recorded as stereo video, yet policies cannot learn from them directly; clinical video carries no action labels. We present FlowScope, which pretrains a surgical policy network on unlabeled clinical video by forecasting scene flow, the metric 3D motion of scene points, with labels computed by off-the-shelf stereo depth and optical flow models. We pretrain the trunk of an Action Chunking Transformer (ACT) on a dataset of 1,149 clinical procedures (1.35M clips) and discard the flow head afterward, so the deployed policy is a standard ACT with no added inference cost. On a real surgical robotic system, FlowScope raises needle pickup success from 54% to 90% and first-handover success on needle regrasp from 4% to 52% (full task: 2% to 14%) over training from scratch (n=50 trials each), and retains its gains with a generative flow-matching action head. Ablations indicate that metric 3D motion is the strongest pretraining signal among those tested; kinematics, 2D tracks, depth alone, and scale-free 3D flow, each paired with kinematics, yield smaller or no gains. Representation analyses suggest that scene-flow pretraining encodes metric needle depth more accurately, that kinematics pretraining increases reliance on the robot state, and that needle-depth probes follow the ordering of real-robot success where offline validation error does not.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Staleness Deadlines for Motion Plans during Onboard Reasoning》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Language models may enable general autonomy in robot planning, but onboard their reasoning can run for most of a minute while the robot and nearby pedestrians keep moving. The robot must then decide how long to follow a plan that the pending answer may overturn. Here we present staleness deadlines, which hand that decision to the robot's clock. Calibrated prediction regions for pedestrians and deviation tubes for the robot set the deadline. When the language model answers, a graph planner rebuilds the route, which the robot adopts only after it passes the same clearance check against walls and predicted pedestrians. Exact binomial tests determine which prespecified budget-selection policies keep close calls, wrong decisions and late arrivals below their targets at each inference profile. Candidate policies either fix one budget or set each query's budget from how long the robot can continue its plan before it must brake. We study corridor signs that close routes and delivery requests whose destination the language model must infer. In 81,000 simulated missions with recorded Qwen3-4B answers, full reasoning cuts wrong decisions about twentyfold, and tracking until the deadline cuts late arrivals by up to 64%. The robot never collides while it moves, although pedestrians who never yield still walk into the stopped robot in 23% of offline and 32% of live missions. On a Jetson AGX Orin under perception load, short budgets fail the wrong-decision target, and full reasoning the lateness target. Between these extremes, a fixed-budget policy passes for corridor signs and per-query policies pass for deliveries. Live Orin runs match offline replay on all three losses in 24 of the 32 missions where either run records a loss. The deadline turns slower reasoning into measurable mission outcomes, which let designers test how much reasoning each inference profile can support.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Coherent_Moment_Propagation_Epistemic_Uncertainty_for_Pre-trained_Robot_Policies|Coherent Moment Propagation: Epistemic Uncertainty for Pre-trained Robot Policies]]

![[assets/2611.26855_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.26855](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26855)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.26855
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Coherent_Moment_Propagation_Epistemic_Uncertainty_for_Pre-trained_Robot_Policies|Coherent Moment Propagation: Epistemic Uncertainty for Pre-trained Robot Policies]]
- **作者**: Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz, Anqi Li, Claudia D'Arpino, Alperen Degirmenci, Tucker Hermans, Fabio Ramos
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, EmbodiedAI

#### 研究背景与动机

《Coherent Moment Propagation: Epistemic Uncertainty for Pre-trained Robot Policies》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Flow matching policies underpin the state-of-the-art in robot learning, with impressive results ranging from long-horizon planning to dexterous manipulation. However, they provide no measure of whether their predicted commands can be trusted, particularly in novel situations outside their training set. Epistemic uncertainty reflects uncertainty about the model's weights and quantifies this ignorance, but calculating it at inference time from a flow matching policy is not trivial. Actions are produced by an ODE solver, requiring uncertainty to be propagated through the entire integration process. BayesDiff addresses this problem for diffusion models by propagating the mean and variance of the state through the denoising chain under a last-layer Laplace posterior approximation, but it severely underestimates the epistemic uncertainty. In this paper we: i) prove that, for flow matching and diffusion ODEs, BayesDiff's total variance decays at rate $O(1/T)$ as the number of Euler steps $T$ used by the solver increases; ii) introduce Coherent Moment Propagation (CoMP), two recursions inspired by the extended and unscented Kalman filters that fix this problem by carrying the cross-covariance between state and weights; and iii) widen the last-layer posterior with observation embeddings the policy already computes, improving uncertainty estimates without retraining for CoMP and several test-time estimators alike. We demonstrate these benefits on three manipulation benchmarks with applications in failure detection and demonstration selection for fine-tuning, where CoMP corrects BayesDiff's variance collapse while matching or outperforming several uncertainty estimators.

</details>

- **作者**: Ihab S. Mohamed, Lantao Liu
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Search-Informed MPPI for Kinodynamic Motion Planning via Monte Carlo Tree Expansion》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MPNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Kinodynamic motion planning in large-scale, cluttered environments remains challenging, as dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories must be computed efficiently over long horizons. Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) can strategically explore long-horizon alternatives but faces large branching factors in continuous control spaces and often relies on random default-policy rollouts, whereas Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) efficiently optimizes local trajectories yet remains susceptible to obstacle-induced local minima. To overcome these challenges, we propose Search-Informed MPPI (SI-MPPI), a unified framework that embeds MPPI trajectory optimization within MCTS-based tree search. The core concept is to fuse the expansion and default-policy simulation stages of MCTS into a single MPPI expansion stage guided by structured intermediate targets, termed baits. During each expansion, an adaptive bait-handling rule retains geometrically valid baits as local targets for GPU-batched MPPI optimizations that generate dynamically feasible candidate motion segments. Feasibility gating then checks these candidates for collision safety and sufficient progress, and top-$k$ selection admits the lowest-cost accepted candidates as tree edges, yielding sparse, directed tree growth; thereafter, effective sample size (ESS)-guided adaptation warm-starts subsequent expansions, while cost-based backpropagation updates tree statistics. Extensive simulations across four large-scale, cluttered 2D occupancy-grid maps demonstrate that SI-MPPI consistently produces the smallest search trees and smoothest angular control profiles compared with three kinodynamic baselines, while maintaining competitive planning times and path lengths. Ablation results further highlight the importance of GPU batching, adaptive bait handling, and retaining multiple expansion candidates for planning efficiency and robustness.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《One Behavior, Out of Order: Deterministic Execution and Parallel Simulation of ROS 2 Applications》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

System-level simulation is widely used to validate robotic applications before hardware deployment. However, Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2), a popular middleware for such applications, uses asynchronous publish-subscribe communication, which can make application behavior depend on scheduling and the platform. The behavior can then vary across runs and machines even with the same inputs. This nondeterminism makes faults hard to reproduce and tests unreliable. In this paper, we first give ROS 2 applications a lightweight coordination scheme in which every component, including event-driven ones, reads its inputs and writes its outputs at predefined time instants. By decoupling computation and communication, the scheme makes an application deterministic, that is, it always has one behavior for given inputs. Tests are thus repeatable, but for large applications they still take a long time. To speed up these tests, we then present an out-of-order parallel simulation algorithm. From the predefined read and write instants, it knows before the run which activations of components depend on which. It starts each activation as soon as those it depends on have finished. Finally, we implement the algorithm in a simulator that runs existing ROS 2 applications without any change to them or to ROS 2. We evaluate the simulator on industrial use cases and synthetic graphs. On the industrial use cases, it is up to 2.6 times faster than synchronous parallel simulation and needs an order of magnitude less memory for messages than asynchronous parallel simulation.

</details>
